# MetaQuotes Certification Program

You think you know MetaTrader 5 inside out and have all the skills needed to operate the platform for your broker successfully? Put your knowledge to the test and become a MetaQuotes certified professional.

- ·How to install and deploy the MetaTrader 5 trading cluster?
- ·How to protect against DDoS attacks and recover after a malfunction?
- ·How to create your own ECN and connect liquidity providers?
- ·How to configure the MetaTrader 5 platform to meet regulatory requirements?
- ·What to do if the quotes have stopped and connection to the trade server has been lost?

These and many other questions arise in day-to-day brokerage activities. The answers are provided in this book, in [article published on the Support Center](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/articles) site and in [the MetaTrader 5 platform documentation](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/docs/mt5). Nevertheless, all this information cannot replace a specialist who knows WHAT to do in a specific situation and WHERE to find the necessary information.

[Pass certification](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/certification)from MetaQuotes to verify your knowledge and get the documentary evidence of your qualification for a broker.

![](_page_3_Picture_10.jpeg)

# Through certification, you will

- ·Test your knowledge
- ·Study relevant material and turn your weak points into strong ones
- ·Learn how to handle the existing functionality in depth
- ·Have an understanding of new product features
- ·Become a certified MetaQuotes professional
- ·Enrich your employment opportunities

## **Three types of certification**

- 1. [MetaTrader 5 Administrator](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/certification/courses/4) for platform administrators
- 2. [MetaTrader 5 Manager](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/certification/courses/6) for client managers
- 3. [MetaTrader 5 Dealer](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/certification/courses/7) for dealers and risk managers

#### **Free online test and two oral exam attempts**

Even if you are not confident in your abilities, start with the first certification stage — the online test. This test is free, while the number of attempts is unlimited. Check out the topics covered by the test and prepare for the certification.

If you feel confident about your knowledge and can easily pass the first stage, it will not be difficult for you to pass the second-stage interview. During the interview, we will additionally verify your knowledge before issuing a certificate. Even if your first interview attempt is unsuccessful, you will have a free retake attempt.

It is safe to say that getting a certificate is easier than obtaining a driver's license provided that you really spent some time behind the wheel and read the traffic code.

We offer one free certification for each company. [Order it now](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/certification) via the Certification section in the MetaQuotes Support Center.

## **You are just a few steps away from becoming a certified professional**

Select the necessary certificate type and pass two stages:

- · Stage 1. Complete the online test consisting of approximately 80 multi-choice questions at your convenience. To complete the test, you should score 70% correct answers. Once you have successfully completed this test, you can then go on to sign up for stage two.
- · Stage 2. Pay for the oral exam and be interviewed by a MetaQuotes representative via a video call. The interview lasts for roughly 30 minutes and verifies that the results of the first stage are legitimate.

Successful participants receive an official certificate signed and verified by a unique 12-digit ID which proves authenticity. The document can be validated by using this [link](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/certification).

Enroll in the program now by going to the [Certification](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/certification)section on the MetaQuotes Support Center website. It takes less than 10 seconds.

# Client onboarding

Fast and easy registration is one of the major prerequisites for maintaining a continuous inflow of new customers.

You should properly configure the [demo account registration option](#page-7-0)as this is an important selling point. By providing the ability to easily try your service, you can encourage traders to proceed to a real account.

The easiest and fastest way to create an account is to request it through the client terminal. The user will only need to fill in a simple form. The broker can start collecting valid user details straight at the demo account registration step. The platform supports [automated verification](#page-29-0)of phone numbers and emails. Thus, you will have valid contact details to connect with potential customers.

It is important not to destroy customer experience by excessive bureaucracy. The client should be able to receive a real account without excessive registration procedures. MetaTrader 5 simplified all account opening processes. Traders can request an account directly from the client terminal, by filling out a short form and uploading an ID and proof of address documents. Furthermore, the broker can enable [automated document](#page-56-0) [verification](#page-56-0)by configuring integration with a KYC service provider. Automated verification speeds up the registration procedure and minimizes manual operations.

MetaTrader 5 also provides the necessary tools to [manage clients](#page-6-0). All registered accounts are automatically linked to client records. Details from registration forms are additionally registered in these records. You can grant different database access permissions for regional offices, appoint responsible managers, work with documents and perform many other tasks straight in the trading platform, without using third-party specialized software.

<span id="page-7-0"></span>

# Allocation of demo accounts

In the financial industry, a person has to overcome a variety of obstacles on their way towards becoming a client. Legal and compliance departments require the fulfilling of vast documentation along with multiple verifications even for the simplest of operations. Such a strict policy often leads to a decrease in customer inflow.

To protect against potential problems, brokers create complex registration and verification schemes for the demo account opening procedure:

- ·the trader needs to enter the site
- ·specify real data
- ·complete phone number and email verification
- ·receive account details and enter them in appropriate fields in the terminal

Such a complicated multi-step account opening procedure definitely reduces the number of potential clients.

A demo account is an important point in attracting new customers. A user can start demo trading without completing KYC procedures. A demo account enables traders to test strategies and practice before proceeding with live trading. By restricting the usage of demo accounts, the potential real clients figures will ultimately drop.

## More accounts — more clients

Enable demo account opening via terminals and attract more potential clients.

Traders are not generally happy when they are prompted to register on a website. They may want to find another broker, allowing them to quickly view conditions and practice trading. Users perform a minimum of steps in the terminal and can start trading on a demo account without completing the KYC procedure.

Conversion rates on your site can be higher from the mathematical point of view, rates which your marketing department can use as a proof of the scheme's operational effectiveness. For example, 2,000 registrations were converted to 50 real accounts. Thus, the conversion rate is at 2.5%. A higher number of registrations can be completed in the terminals, for example 50,000. Even with a conversion rate of 0.5% this would create 250 real accounts. More potential customers at the input generate more real accounts at the output.

Do not be afraid of a large number of accounts. The high performance of the MetaTrader 5 platform allows you to service millions of demo accounts on one trading server without the need of purchasing additional licenses.

Do not worry about how to process unverified accounts. The platform features push notifications and an internal email system, which allow the delivery of promotional materials directly to terminals.

## Demo group settings

Platform configuration starts with groups, in which accounts will be created. The groups define basic operation conditions, such as risk management models, swap and commission charges, authorization types, permissions and others. Types of accounts displayed in the registration form on the client terminal side are created based on the groups.

Open MetaTrader 5 Administrator and create groups. The names of demo groups should begin with "demo".

Create separate groups for clients from different countries to enable automatic distribution of registrations for regional sales departments. Set the availability of accounts depending on users' location, and all registrations will be distributed by groups at the account opening stage. Your employees with appropriate positions will then manage these groups.

Specify the account deposit currency. Align it with the country, for which the group will be used.

## Account allocation settings

Create an account type based on the newly created group; after that it will become available in client terminals. Go to "Clients & Accounts \ Allocations" and create a new record.

You cannot add a disabled group to the list of groups for opening demo accounts. Allocation of demo accounts is impossible in such groups. If there is a disabled group among the previously selected ones, a warning is displayed when closing the dialog window.

Specify the company accordingly to define in which terminals the group will be available. If you are not working with affiliates, leave the Default value. If you work with several White Labels, then specify the name of your company. Create separate groups and account types for your affiliates. This will help you organize work with demo registrations.

We also recommend configuring at least one default demo group. This will enable users to open accounts on your server from any broker's terminal.

Specify the name of the account type associated with this group. Use a short and clear name as it will be displayed in client terminals.

Specify a list of countries in which the account type will be available. Use this feature to assign registrations to the relevant sales departments. Create separate account types for Europe, Asia and America. This will automatically filter the incoming flow of accounts by different groups at the registration stage.

The user's country is determined automatically on the client terminal side. Specify a comma separated list of available leverage options. The first value in the list will be used as a default leverage.

Up to two types of agreements can be displayed in a normal account opening form:

- · Client Agreement (or Client Agreement and Risk Notification) and Data Protection Policy, in case both agreement types are specified. If the Client Agreement is not specified, the first user agreement from the list (if any) will be shown instead. A user agreement is the one, which name has been specified manually (rather than selected from the predefined list).
- · Client Agreement (or Client Agreement and Risk Notification) or Data Protection Policy, in case only one of them is specified.
- · If no agreements are specified, only the following check box will be displayed in the account opening dialog: "I agree with the account opening terms and the data protection policy".

Up to ten types of agreements are shown in the expanded form.

Agreements can be marked as mandatory. For example, the acceptance of account opening rules and Privacy Policy is required by law and is therefore considered as mandatory. Such agreements require a separate, explicitly set checkbox in the account registration window, while the acceptance of the agreement will be additionally displayed in the trade server log.

When the user accepts each mandatory agreement, a relevant event is added to the trade server log:

```
'27904969': accepts mandatory agreement 'Risk disclosure' -
https://abcbroker.com/legal/risk

```
Accepts agreements on account '1092' allocation :
Client agreement
Risk disclosure
```

See also: [Articles / Add agreement links to account registration forms](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/articles/1570)

We also recommend enabling the option "Automatic allocation of demo account on first start". In this case, a demo account will be created automatically for the client during the first launch of the client terminal. The account will be created in the first group available for this terminal/company.

This feature can have a positive effect on the conversion, since users are allowed to skip the registration stage. Fast acquaintance with the platform will further encourage traders to request live accounts.

## Segmentation of accounts by groups

The distributed architecture of the trading platform enables the management of a huge number of accounts, without compromising performance. Performance optimization is supported both at the cluster level and at the database level.

To ensure efficient server operation, it is recommended not to use huge groups that include several thousand accounts. Instead, it is better to create several smaller groups and to evenly distribute accounts among them. This will improve the quality of your services.

The platform provides the automated distribution of accounts into different groups, at the moment the account is opened via a client terminal.

To enable this feature, create account groups with indexes in the name, such as:

- ·demo\forex-hedge-usd-01
- ·demo\forex-hedge-usd-02
- ·demo\forex-hedge-usd-03

These groups must have the same settings.

Next, in the account allocation settings, specify the group as follows: demo\forex-hedge-usd-01..03.

All accounts will be evenly distributed into three groups.

Distribution of accounts is performed by access servers. Accounts are distributed evenly, but not necessarily sequentially, i.e. not necessarily from the first to the last group in the list.

## Server settings

Common demo account operation parameters are specified in the trading server settings, in the "Network Cluster" section. Open the Demo tab and make sure demo account allocation is allowed. We recommend renewing the account lifetime after each connection. Deletion of accounts after an expiration period can cause a negative reaction from users, who lose their trading statistics.

![](_page_14_Picture_6.jpeg)

## Checking the settings

Now, open the client terminal and check demo account registration. Required leverage options and agreement links should be available in the form. Also, check the availability of account types corresponding to the country. Open the Administrator terminal to make sure that the account is created in the appropriate group.

All new accounts will be automatically created in different groups in accordance with the user's country. Thus, configure access permissions to regional managers accordingly.

## Client management system

All trading accounts are linked to the appropriate [client records](#page-32-0), which store traders' data automatically collected by the MetaTrader 5 platform, such as personal information, addresses, documents and trading accounts.

When a trader opens an account via the terminal, the platform compares the specified name, e-mail and phone with the existing database. If a client with matching data is found, the account is [linked](#page-35-0) to the found record.

Additionally, missing data is automatically copied to the account. If the corresponding client is not found, a new record is created automatically, and related information is copied to it.

The same mechanism is used for real accounts requested from terminals. Data specified in the extended registration form, including documents, is automatically added to the client record. As a result, you will not have to copy data manually. After [document verification,](#page-48-0) you will only need to move the account from a preliminary group to the live one.

Publicly distribute terminals and demo accounts rather than hiding them in traders' rooms. This will help you attract more clients. Add [mobile terminal deep links](#page-119-0) in your sites and registration emails. This will increase functionality and enhance operations efficiency with account opening.

# Allocation of preliminary accounts

Novice traders are generally conquered with doubts. When a beginner decides to start real trading, it is important to open a trading account without going through the burdensome bureaucratic procedures. Ensuring a simple and fast account opening procedure is essential in retaining your clients.

The client terminal is the trader's main workplace, from which most of the operations are performed. However, brokers often register live accounts only through the website, which means that users need to find the resource and fill out additional forms. We offer brokers to take the advantage of MetaTrader 5 and provide traders with the possibility to request live accounts directly from the client terminal by filling out a simple form. Offer this option to your clients and increase your conversion rate!

## Simplify account management

Set up an automated real account opening process. The data specified by the traders in the registration form is delivered straight to the platform. Ultimately, you do not have to create an account, copy information from the trader's room using plug-ins or enter it manually via the Manager terminal. An account can be simply moved from a preliminary group to the live one after data verification.

In this case, the trader completes a usual registration similar to the demo account opening procedure. If possible, the terminal automatically adds data from previous requests to the form, thus simplifying the process as a whole.

## Access the full history of traders

MetaTrader 5 maintains a complete history of interactions with the trader and stores all related data in a single client record.

When requesting a live account, the trader fills in the extended form and uploads the ID and address confirmation documents. This information is saved in the client record. A trading account is also linked to this record. You no longer need to collect data from various sources, since the platform collects information automatically.

### KYC in mobile terminals

Live account requests can also be sent from [mobile terminals for iPhone and Android](#page-115-0). The trader fills in the form, takes document photos, submits data and opens an account. You do not need to additionally configure your mobile platforms: set up the registration of real accounts once, and this option will be available to all Windows, Android and iOS users. Get closer to your potential customers — the number of smartphone users is growing daily.

![](_page_21_Picture_1.jpeg)

### Preliminary groups

Live accounts requested via client terminals are created with the zero balance, in special [preliminary groups](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/docs/mt5/platform/administration/admin_groups/groups_settings). Trading is disabled in such groups.

Open MetaTrader 5 Administrator and create preliminary groups for different countries, currencies and account types. This will enable automatic distribution of registrations to regional sales departments and will allow clients to select desired trading conditions, for example to request a standard, mini or micro account.

The names of preliminary groups should begin with "preliminary". Specify other settings similar to demo account configurations.

Make sure to restart the trading platform after creating groups, to enable opening of accounts in the new groups.

#### Account allocation settings

To make a group available in client terminals, create an account type based on the new group in the Allocations section. Set parameters (similar to the demo groups):

- ·Specify the company and the account type name
- ·Set availability by countries to distribute requests by sales departments
- · Configure agreements (up to 8) to be accepted by traders during registration, to simplify further the KYC procedure

Enable employment, income and experience fields if such information is required by your regulator. Additional steps for specifying appropriate data will be added to the registration form. The collected information will be saved in appropriate client records.

If you cannot request documents during account registration, you can disable this function. This will cause the corresponding fields to be hidden in the form.

#### Configuring welcome emails

After requesting a real account, the trader receives a standard [welcome email](#page-72-0)which contains preliminary account credentials and a note that the trader will soon be contacted by a manager to complete the registration process.

Replace default emails with your own messages containing the desired information. Email templates are stored on the trading server, in the \templates\greeting\preliminary directory. They are available as HTML files containing macros.

Individual emails can be configured for your partners. Templates can be set separately for each account group: create a subdirectory with templates and specify its name in group settings.

![](_page_24_Picture_2.jpeg)

## Checking the settings

You can now open the client terminal and check preliminary account registration. The form should contain agreement links and all fields selected during customization. Furthermore, you can check the availability of account types corresponding to the relevant country and delivery of welcome emails. Then, open the Administrator terminal and check registration on the server side.

New accounts will be automatically created in appropriate groups in accordance with the user's country — verify data and move them to the real groups.

### The real account is almost ready: client management

The platform automatically collects relevant information about traders. Every opened account is [linked to the](#page-35-0) [appropriate client record.](#page-35-0) All data including documents are stored in a single place.

Assign managers and compliance department specialists to process incoming account requests. Your employees can maintain the traders' history, update data and change statuses straight out of the platform. The system will monitor and display all changes in the versions section, allowing control over all managers' actions.

[Customizable filters](#page-177-0) are available in the Manager terminal for a more convenient operation. Any employee can view assigned clients or users from a selected country. Filter settings are stored on the trading server and linked to the manager account, so the required tools can be accessed from any computer.

# Verification of contact details

The trading platform provides built-in features for the verification of phone numbers and emails which traders specify when opening [demo](#page-7-0) and [preliminary](#page-17-0) accounts via client terminals. Configure verification and access wider opportunities in working with potential clients by collecting valid contact information.

## Verification setup

Small confirmation codes are sent to traders by SMS and email to verify the data.

Before you enable SMS and email options in the platform, the system administrator should configure platform integration with messengers and email services.

Once done, open "Clients & Accounts \ Allocations" in MetaTrader 5 Administrator. In the Confirm parameter of each account type, select which data you want to be verified: phone number, email or both.

Specify the email server which will be used to confirm email addresses during the registration of specific account types.

When sending a confirmation SMS, the platform will choose the best available provider. The platform takes into account the client group and country, as well as the provider operation statistics. This allows for more reliable delivery as well as lower costs for provider services.

You can additionally specify your own email and SMS text to be sent to traders for confirmation. Use standard templates on the trade server:

- ·[main server installation directory]\templates\verify\_email — HTM files with email templates.
- · [main server installation directory]\templates\verify\_phone — text files for SMS templates. Do not use too long messages. If the allowed length is exceeded (depending on the provider), a message may be cropped or split into multiple SMS.

See also:

- ·[Documentation / Platform / Platform settings / Integration / Mail servers](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/docs/mt5/platform/administration/integration/integration_mail)
- ·[Documentation / Platform / Platform settings / Integration / Messengers](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/docs/mt5/platform/administration/integration/integration_messenger)

## Confirmation on the client side

During account registration, traders provide their contact data, including phone number and email.

If verification is enabled for the selected account type (group), confirmation codes are automatically sent to the trader and special code fields appear in the dialog box.

Confirmation codes are valid for three hours. If the code is not entered in the field within this time frame, the trader will need to repeat the procedure.

Before sending codes, the system checks whether the specified phone/email has been previously confirmed. If the trader has already passed verification from their computer in the last 72 hours, an account will be opened without additional confirmation. Thus, there will be no additional burden for traders and no additional costs for brokers.

The system automatically controls the correctness of specified phone numbers on the client terminal side. This prevents the sending of codes to incorrect numbers and thus saves you costs for the SMS provider services.

- · The phone number must be specified in the international format: +[country code][number], for example: +79171113594. The number is indicated without spaces.
- ·This must be a mobile phone number, not landline.

In addition, the system checks the correctness of email addresses. Temporary addresses and unreliable mail services are not allowed.

The number of confirmations of different phone numbers and email addresses that a trader can request from one computer is limited for security purposes. When the limit is reached, confirmation codes are no longer sent, and the following messages is printed in the server log:

too many phone confirmation requests from IP too many email confirmation requests from IP

<span id="page-32-0"></span>

# Clients

The Client is a separate entity in the trading platform. This is the aggregate information on a trader, including [personal data,](#page-39-0) [documents](#page-48-0) and [trading accounts.](#page-54-0) The client record stores the history of all changes: every version made by any manager is available in the [Versions](#page-53-0) section.

All accounts registered in the platform are automatically connected to an existing or a new client. Thus, the client entity conveniently stores all accounts of a trader, even those opened in different groups or having different types. This data arrangement registers all client performed actions before opening a real account and allows evaluation of the client's real LTV (Life-Time Value).

All clients are displayed in a separate section:

The context menu contains commands for managing the Clients section:

·Displayed data is managed by the Columns command

- ·The [list of clients](#page-177-0) can be managed using the Filters submenu
- ·[Information to clients](#page-64-0) can be sent using the E-Mail command
- ·Details on clients' operations can be retrieved from the trade server journal by the Journal command

If Autoscroll is enabled, the list of clients is scrolled to the last one whenever a new trading account is added to the list. The auto scroll can only be applied to a list sorted by the identifier (ID).

The manager account needs to have appropriate permissions for accessing the Clients section. Such permissions are provided by the platform administrator.

### Creating clients automatically and manually

All accounts registered in the platform via client terminals are [automatically linked to clients](#page-35-0). If there is no client record for the newly created account, such a record is created automatically. All information from the account is copied to the client record. Minimum details are required for demo accounts: name, surname, company, email and phone number.

More data can be requested during the registration of preliminary accounts via client terminals. Depending on platform settings, the following data may be requested:

- ·Detailed personal data
- ·ID and proof-of-address documents
- ·Nationality and tax ID number
- ·Social status, employment and education
- ·Income and its sources
- ·Trading experience in various financial markets

If you enable the extended account registration form, all the above data will be automatically added to client records. Thus, you will not need to additionally request this information and add it manually to the database.

To create a client manually, click "New client" in the context menu of the client list or press Ctrl+Shift+N.

After that specify required information in sections [Personal data,](#page-39-0) [Address](#page-44-0) and [Regulation](#page-47-0). Once you save the client record, document and trading account management sections will appear.

When a [trading account is created](#page-184-0) via the Manager terminal, an appropriate client record is not created automatically. To create a record manually based on account data, open the account dialog and select "New client" in the lower left corner. To create an account linked to a client record, use the [Trading](#page-54-0) [accounts](#page-54-0) section in the appropriate client record.

<span id="page-35-0"></span>

#### Binding accounts to clients and copying data

The trading platform automatically binds each trading account opened via the client terminal to a client record. Search is based on the comparison of trading account data with data in client records:

- · First, a client with the matching First Name, Last Name and E-Mail is searched (all the three should match, case insensitive).
- · If no such client is found, the system searches for a client with the matching First Name, Last Name and phone number (all the three fields should match, case insensitive).

If no suitable record is found, a new client is created, and the trading account is linked to it.

After that the account data is copied to the client record in accordance with priority. Demo accounts and client records created based on demo accounts have the lowest priority. The highest priority is given to data from real accounts and manually created clients.

- · **Demo account** — when binding such an account to an existing client record, which was created earlier on the basis of another demo account, only missing data is copied.
- · **Preliminary account** — when binding such an account to an existing client record, which was created earlier on the basis of a demo account, all data is copied (existing data is overwritten). If the client was created on the basis of a preliminary account, only missing data is copied. If the client was created on the basis of a real account or was added manually, data is not copied.
- · **Real account** — when binding such an account to an existing client record, which was created earlier on the basis of a preliminary or demo account, all data is copied (existing data is overwritten). If the client was created on the basis of a real account or was added manually, data is not copied.

If the account is linked to a newly created record, all account data is copied.

The relevant details are also copied to clients after each time the account is changed. In this case, the data is only copied to the empty fields of the client. For example, when the manager adds an address to account data. If the address is not filled for the client to whom the account is bound, the data will be copied to the relevant fields.

<span id="page-36-0"></span>

#### Differentiation of access to clients for managers

A client record is available to the manager if one of the following conditions is met:

- ·The client has been created by this manager
- ·The client is explicitly [assigned](#page-38-0) to the manager
- · [The preferred trading group](#page-38-1) set for the client is available to the manager (the manager has permissions for the group)
- · Any of the [trading accounts bound to the client](#page-54-0) is available to the manager (the manager has permissions for the group in which the account is located)

# General client information

This section displays general information about the client, as well as the responsible employees who manage the client.

|                              | Update                            |

- <span id="page-37-0"></span>· **Type** — individual or corporate client. The Company tab is additionally available for corporate clients, in which company details are specified.
- · **Status** sets the client status:
  - ·Not registered — an anonymous client created based on a demo account without any data.
  - ·Registered — the client has created a demo account and has filled contact details.
  - · Not interested — the client has created a demo account and has filled contact details but is not interested in opening a real account.

- ·Not completed — the client has provided data for opening a real account.
- · Completed — the client has provided data for opening a real account and has submitted all the required documents.
- ·Information — to open a real account, the client needs to provide additional information.
- ·Rejected — the client was denied registration.
- ·Approved — a real account has been opened for the client.
- ·Funded — the client has deposited money to a real account.
- ·Active — the client has trading activity.
- ·Inactive — the client does not have any trading activity.
- ·Suspended — work with the client has been suspended.
- ·Closed — the client has left at their own decision.
- ·Terminated — work with the client was terminated at the initiative of the company.
- <span id="page-38-2"></span>·**KYC status** — the result of a client check via the [KYC](#page-41-0)service.
- <span id="page-38-0"></span>· **Assigned manager** — the name of the responsible employee. To find all the clients managed by a certain employee, enable the "Assigned manager" column in the client list and sort the list by this column. The list of managers available for selection is formed in accordance with the settings of the manager who is viewing the client. If the current manager account is linked to a certain company on the trading server, the available list will only include managers from the same company. A manager without any company specified can access all managers on the server. If the required managers are not available to you in the list, please contact your trading platform administrator.
- <span id="page-38-1"></span>· **Preferred trading group** — the field is used to [distribute client records](#page-36-0) between manager accounts. If this field is empty, the client record will be available to managers regardless of their account group permissions. If filled, the client will only be available to the managers having the specified group permissions.
- · **Approved by** — different employees can be responsible for the creation and final approval of clients. For example, a manager can register a client, fill in necessary data and set the Registered status. Then a compliance specialist may verify the data, change the status to Approved, set the approval date and specify his account in the "Approved by" field.
- · **Approval date** — data and document verification and approval date. The date is set by the data approving specialist.
- ·**Close date** — the date when the provision of services to the client was terminated.
- · **Lead Source, Lead Campaign** — the website from which the client came (lead source) and the name of the marketing campaign, as a result of which the client came.

These fields enable the analysis of marketing campaigns and tracking of leads. To receive the data, add the special parameters to the client or mobile platform download links:

```
https://download.mql5.com/cdn/web/metaquotes.ltd/mt5/mt5setup.exe?
utm_source=YourWebsite&utm_campaign=YourCampaign
https://download.mql5.com/cdn/mobile/mt5/ios?server=ABC-Demo,ABC-
Real&utm_source=YourWebsite&utm_campaign=YourCampaign
https://download.mql5.com/cdn/mobile/mt5/android?server=ABC-Demo,ABC-
Real&utm_source=YourWebsite&utm_campaign=YourCampaign
```

Here YourCampaign is the company name, and YourWebsite is the address of the website hosting the link. In the 'server' parameter of the mobile platform links, enter the list of your servers to be shown to traders when they open an account.

When opening a demo account and connecting to any trading account via the terminal downloaded using such a link, utm\_source and utm\_campaign values will be registered in the client record on the server side. If the fields are already filled, the label values will not be overwritten when the account reconnects (even when connecting from a terminal downloaded by a link with other labels).

- ·**Introducer** — the login (trading account) of the user, who introduced the client.
- · **Visitor ID** — a unique identifier assigned to the user when they install your terminal or visit your site, if a Finteza tracker is installed in it. With this tracker, you can trace trader behavior, from the first website visit to a real account deposit. For further details please visit the [Analytics](#page-90-0) section.

Lead Source and Lead Campaign data from terminal download links is registered in created accounts only if you have a [Finteza license](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/docs/mt5/platform/administration/integration/integration_finteza).

If you wish to process client data using third-party software, you can save the relevant data to a file. Click Export and select the data which should be exported to a file: documents, history, etc. After that click Save:

| Report parts       |               |                     |           |              |              |
| √ Client           |               | Documents           | Comments  |              |              |
| ⥱ Trading Accounts |               | ∨ Files             | ☐ History |              |              |
|                    |               |                     |           |              | ±            |

- · **External ID** — client ID in an external trading system. This field can be used for integrations and for generating reports using API.

<span id="page-41-0"></span>

## KYC Check

The trading platform integration with KYC (Know Your Customer) services enables the validation of client personal data and [documents](#page-48-0) with one click or even in a fully automated mode.

The trader [requests a live account](#page-17-0) via the desktop or mobile terminal by filling out a form and uploading documents:

| Open an Account        |                                                                                                                                         |                                                                                                                                                               | ?                    | ×      |
|------------------------|-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|----------------------|--------|
| Opening a real account | A real account is required for live trading on financial markets                                                                        |                                                                                                                                                               |                      |        |
| State:                 | New York                                                                                                                                |                                                                                                                                                               |                      |        |
| City:                  | New York                                                                                                                                | 123456                                                                                                                                                        |                      |        |
| Zip code:              | 123456                                                                                                                                  |                                                                                                                                                               |                      |        |
| Address:               | Wall st, 1                                                                                                                              |                                                                                                                                                               |                      |        |
| Identity:              | <b>ID Card</b>                                                                                                                          | Please take photos of your documents and upload them here without any editing.<br>image or PDF file for the front side<br>image or PDF file for the back side | $\cdots$<br>$\cdots$ |        |
| Address:               | Utility bill                                                                                                                            | image or PDF file                                                                                                                                             | $\sqrt{1}$           |        |
|                        | - Some types of documents require two photos: front and back side<br>- Bills for mobile services are not accepted as a proof of address | - Utility bill must contain your name and address and be no older than three months                                                                           |                      |        |
|                        |                                                                                                                                         | $\sqrt{1}$ agree with the terms and conditions for opening an account and the data protection policy                                                          |                      |        |
|                        |                                                                                                                                         | < Back<br>Next                                                                                                                                                |                      | Cancel |

A preliminary account with a zero balance is created for the client. This account along with the relevant data and documents is linked to the client record. The manager clicks the "KYC Check" button in the [client personal data](#page-39-0) section and the platform automatically sends a request to the KYC system.

The KYC system checks data and documents and returns the result, which the manager can view in the platform as a report:

#### Documents

This section allows management of client's documents. Here you can add, edit or delete documents. Documents provided by the client during preliminary account opening are automatically added to this section.

The flexible system of permissions enables multi-level processing of documents.

For example, a customer department employee creates a new record and adds documents to it. Further, a compliance specialist may download and check documents, and then set the appropriate status, such as 'approved' (for example, when the client confirms the authenticity of documents by visiting the office), 'rejected' (for example, if the document expired), etc.

All document status changes are displayed in the version history.

The list shows general information about documents: unique document ID in the platform, type, etc. To view more information about the document, double-click on its line in the list. To view the document, click .

#### Adding and editing documents

To add a document, click "New" at the bottom of the section, to edit a document double click on it in the list. General information is shown in the Documents tab:

- ·**Created by** — the name of the manager who has created the document, along with the creation date.
- · **Last changed by** — the name of the manager who has made the latest changes to the document, and the date of the changes.
- ·**Approved by** — the name of the manager who approved the document.

- · **Status** — the current status of the document: new, approved, rejected, archived, deleted. When the status is changed to Approved, the name of the manager who approved the document and the date of approval are written to the "Approved by" field.
- ·**Document type** — ID, proof of address, legal address, company registration, etc.
- · **Document subtype** — a more specific document type: passport, driver's license, utility bill, bank statement, etc.
- ·**Issue date** — the date when the document was issued.
- ·**Expiration date** — document expiration date.
- · **Name** — the name of the document file. Documents provided by the client during preliminary account registration via terminals are automatically named according to the following rule: [Second Name] [First Name].[Document Type].[Extension]. The document type for proof of identity documents is 'Person', for proof of address it is 'Address'.
- · **Comment** — an arbitrary comment. For example, here you can specify the reason for rejecting the document.

#### **Document comments**

You can add here comments on client documents. If a document is not accepted, you can add instructions to managers to contact the client.

All messages from this section are also displayed in the general list of [client comments.](#page-52-0)

![](_page_52_Figure_1.jpeg)

### <span id="page-52-0"></span>**Comments**

This section stores the client history and comments of all managers working with the client. For example, sales managers can keep a call history, and the supervisory department can leave instructions to managers to contact a client if documents are not in order.

Several types of comments are supported for convenience and efficiency: general comments, log records, calls and automatic records. Choose the appropriate type for your messages, and you will be able to easily identify them in general correspondence.

### Trading accounts

This section contains information about the client's trading accounts, including demo, preliminary and real ones.

All accounts in the trading platform are automatically connected to clients. If an existing client opens a demo or preliminary account via the terminal, this account will be immediately added to the appropriate client record. If this is a new client, a new client record will be created, to which the newly opened account will be connected.

Also, this section allows you to [open accounts](#page-184-0) for the client. To do this, select "New Account" in the context menu. The created account will be automatically connected to the client record. To edit an existing account, double-click on it in the list. After that, all account [parameters](#page-189-0) will be shown in the right window part.

Using this menu, you can bind or unbind any other accounts:

- · Click "Link \ By login" in the context menu. After that a new row will be added at the end of the table. Specify here the number of the account to bind.
- · Select an account and click "Unlink" in the context menu. After confirmation, the selected account will no longer be bound to the client and will be removed from the list.

<span id="page-56-0"></span>

# Automatic KYC checks

The trading platform integration with KYC (Know Your Customer) services enables the validation of client personal data and documents with one click or even in a fully automated mode. Set up connection to an external KYC provider in just a few minutes in order to:

- · Send data and documents for automated verification straight from the client dialog. A detailed report is generated for each verification, which is saved in the client history. Based on the check results, an appropriate status is automatically set for client documents.
- · Configure [automatic data check](#page-60-0) for preliminary account registration. This feature provides the further automation of client onboarding procedures.

[Request trial from Sum & Substance](mailto:metatrader@sumsub.com) to evaluate the KYC procedure benefits.

## Integration Setup

Open the "Integrations — KYC" section and create a new configuration:

Specify the following parameters:

·**Name** — configuration name.

- · **Type** — KYC check service type. Each provider has an individual set of settings. Please request the required settings from the provider. Currently only the [Sum & Substance](https://sumsub.com/) provider is supported. Support for [World-](https://www.refinitiv.com/en/products/world-check-kyc-screening)[Check](https://www.refinitiv.com/en/products/world-check-kyc-screening), [eSpear](https://espear.com/) and other providers will be added soon.
- · **Default KYC provider** — the platform uses this option when [selecting a KYC provider](#page-60-1) to verify client data if verification could not be performed based on group or country settings.

Further settings are provider-dependent. Please request the required parameters from your provider.

#### Sum & Substance

[Sum&Substance](https://sumsub.com/) provides an all-in-one technical and legal toolkit to cover KYC/KYB/AML needs. It is a single powerful platform to convert more customers, speed up the verification processes, reduce costs and detect digital fraud.

To get server access, please fill in the registration form at [https://sumsub.com.](https://sumsub.com) Next, log into the control panel using the provided login and password. The control panel features the required integration data.

![](_page_57_Figure_7.jpeg)

Open the [DevSpace\App Tokens](https://api.sumsub.com/checkus#/devSpace/appTokens) section and add a new application. Add the following permissions for the application: seePersonalData, SeeBgCheckResults, managePersonalData, manageUsers, manageClientSettings, seeStats, seeSensitiveData. After adding the application, you will receive a token and a secret key which you should specify in the provider settings on the MetaTrader 5 side.

Next, create a provider configuration and specify the following parameters:

- · **Provider address** — address for connecting to the service. Default is api.sumsub.com. In most cases, there is no need to change it.
- ·**Token** — token for authorization. The token is available in the [DevSpace](https://api.sumsub.com/checkus#/devSpace/appTokens) section of the control panel.
- · **Secret key** — a secret key for authorization. The secret key is available in the [DevSpace](https://api.sumsub.com/checkus#/devSpace/appTokens) section of the control panel.

<span id="page-58-0"></span>In the "Level name" parameter, specify the name of the setting which defines your client verification process.

Sum & Substance allows the customization of the number of steps which the user must complete in order to obtain the verified status, as well as of the set of required documents and other parameters. This procedure is called the Verification Level.

You can use different levels depending on certain conditions. For example, you can set soft verification for safer regions. Pre-configure verification levels in the Sum & Substance control panel and create several KYC configurations on the MetaTrader 5 side. Specify in the configurations specific verification levels and define the list of countries for which they will be used.

For further details about how to set up verification levels please view the [Sum & Substance documentation.](https://help.sumsub.com/articles/57128-customizing-verification-levels-and-flows)

<span id="page-59-0"></span>

## Countries

Specify the list of countries, for which this KYC provider will be used, for manualand for [automatic](#page-60-0) checks. Use of different providers ensures the best verification conditions for clients from different regions. You can also [adjust](#page-58-0) [the verification process](#page-58-0) depending on the country.

When opening a demo account, the country is determined automatically on the client terminal side. When opening a preliminary account, the country is also detected automatically, but the client can specify another country in the registration form.

<span id="page-60-1"></span>

#### How the provider is selected

- ·The system checks the client group and country
- · The provider located higher in the list is selected from all providers, which meet the [country](#page-59-0) and [group](#page-59-1) conditions
- · If none of the providers is suitable, the default provider is utilized (as set by the "Default KYC provider" option in common settings)

<span id="page-60-0"></span>

[Live accounts](#page-17-0) can be requested from desktop and mobile terminals. Configure the registration form to request supporting documents from clients during registration. <span id="page-62-0"></span>

# Common errors

- ·Do not disable the registration of demo accounts via client terminals. This feature brings in leads.
- · Do not disable the ability to request real accounts from client terminals. By simplifying the client onboarding procedures, you increase conversions.
- · Do not use only one SMS provider to verify phone numbers. We recommend configuring at least two providers to ensure the delivery of confirmation codes, so that you do not lose traders during registration due to undelivered SMS.

<span id="page-63-0"></span>

# Communication with traders

Reach your clients directly, convert demo accounts into real ones, and keep your clients connected and up to date. Offer them the best customer service experience and increase deposits using built-in communication channels in MetaTrader 5.

It is a fact that the brokerage industry has become more competitive than ever. Brokers can no longer rely only on the trading technology and trading conditions in their offerings. Now, the success of a brokerage company depends even more on their clients' loyalty and trust.

This trust can be gained through communication and transparency, which can be achieved through direct communication with the client.

The MetaTrader 5 already features built-in and ready-to-use communication solutions for your specific brokerage business needs:

- · Internal mail. Reach your traders directly via client terminals. You can deliver information even when you do not have valid contact details.
- · Email. Integrate with mail servers and send emails to your traders directly through manager terminals, without using third-party email clients. Furthermore, the integration enables confirmation of email addresses during registration.
- · Push notifications provide a modern alternative to SMS. Messages are delivered to traders' mobile devices via the Internet.
- · Welcome emails. During account opening, the traders automatically receive messages with connection details and useful information. Customize these emails to send the relevant information to your clients.
- · SMS. The SMS functionality can be used for the instant verification of clients' mobile numbers upon new account opening, as well as for sending custom SMS to your clients.
- ·Chat. Offer customer support straight in client terminals via the built-in Chat service.
- ·Company website. Advertise your services and publish information in the frequently visited terminal area.

See also: [Articles / All-in-one communication channels in MetaTrader 5](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/articles/993)

<span id="page-64-0"></span>

# Internal mailing system and emails

Using the internal mailing system, you can deliver any information to your traders: new service promotions, important announcement and so on. To deliver the information you do not need the treader's contact information. Internal mailing system is built into desktop and mobile terminals.

The message will not stay unnoticed: all mails are displayed right in the terminal and are accompanied with sound alerts. The mailing system provides an efficient feedback channel: the trader can reply to mails and request further details.

When using the platform, you can easily send emails to traders without having to launch an external email client.

To send an email, select "Internal mail/Email" in the account menu. For bulk mailing, you can first select all the required account in the list. Their logins will be included in the "To" field.

![](_page_64_Picture_6.jpeg)

Fill in the following fields in the email:

- · **To** — account number of the email recipient. The terminal allows [sending emails to multiple clients](#page-65-0) [simultaneously](#page-65-0).
- ·**Subject** — email subject.
- · **Template** — any email can be saved as a [template](#page-66-0) using the context menu. For example, you can save a typical maintenance work email as a template. To get a ready-made email afterwards, simply select the template in this field.
- ·**Internal mail** — select this option to send a message using the internal mailing system.
- · **Email** — use this option to send an email. The address specified in [account personal data](#page-189-0) will be used. Please note:
  - · A mail server must be configured in the platform to enable email sending. If the function is not available, please contact your platform administrator.
  - · Messages sent by email are not displayed under the "Toolbox \ Mail" section. To check the sending status, select your server in the Navigator and go to the Journal section. If an email has been sent successfully, the recipient address and the configuration name of the mail server through which the email has been sent, are shown in the log: email to someaddress@mail.net sent [MailServer].

By selecting on the toolbar, you can attach a file. Please mind the following attachment restrictions:

- ·One file size should not exceed 8 MB
- ·The total size of attachments should not exceed 16 MB
- ·Up to five files can be attached

To enable the emailing features, the relevant mailbox name must be specified in the manager account on the trade server. Also, the manager should have sufficient permissions. Contact the platform administrator to configure the account.

The mail text editor provides commands for creating lists and inserting images, links and tables. To view or edit the source HTML code of the message, select on the toolbar.

The context menu of the text editing window contains standard commands for working with the text: Copy, Cut, Paste, Insert Hyperlink, Insert Table and Insert Image. From the context menu, you can also work with email [templates](#page-66-0) and [macros](#page-66-1).

<span id="page-65-0"></span>

### Bulk mailing

The manager terminal allows you to send messages to groups of users. The list of users is specified in the "To" field of the email.

·**Multiple Logins** — specify several usernames separated by a comma. For example, 1000, 1001, 1002.

- · **Range of Logins** — send mails to a range of logins, specify the start and end logins of the range through a hyphen. For example, 1000-9000.
- · **Group of Accounts** — to send mails to a certain group, specify the following construction: "group:group name". For example, to send emails to the demoforex group, specify group:demoforex in the "To" field. You can also specify several groups, separated separating them by commas.
- · **Country of Residence** — emails can be sent to traders from a certain country. To do this, specify the following construction: "country:country name" in the "To" field. For example, to send mails to all clients who live in Russia, specify country:Russia. You can also specify several countries, separated by commas.
- · **City of Residence —** to send mails to clients from certain cities, specify "city:city name". You can specify several cities separated by commas.

In the "To" field, you can specify several delivery parameters separated by a semi colon (;). In this case the email will be sent to all the clients who match at least one of the specified parameters. For example, if you specify "group:demoforex, managers; city:Moscow; 1000-2000", emails will be sent to all clients from 'demoforex' and 'managers' groups, all clients who live in Moscow and to all of the clients from the accounts range from 1000 to 2000 inclusive.

When specifying the mailing parameters, you can use the negation sign "!". It allows excluding some of clients from mailing by a certain parameter. For example, if you specify "group:!managers", then messages will be sent to all the users except the ones who belong to the managers group.

When sending an email, the system checks whether there are any clients with the specified delivery parameters. If there are no such clients, the email will not be sent. In this case a corresponding message will appear in the journal.

<span id="page-66-0"></span>

#### Templates

To make working with email easier, use pre-configured email templates. The templates are HTML files which contain macros and enable email customization to address each recipient.

Use the email creation window context menu to work with templates.

All email templates are stored in the /templates/mail folder of the Manager terminal.

<span id="page-66-1"></span>

#### Macros

Using the Macros submenu of the context menu of the email creation window, you can insert macros in the text. They allow substituting different data depending on the email recipient.

- ·Login (#LOGIN#) — email/message recipient's account number.
- ·Name (#USERNAME#) — recipient's first and last name.
- ·Currency (#USER\_CURRENCY#) — recipient's deposit currency.

- ·Balance (#USER\_BALANCE#) — recipient's balance.
- ·Credit (#USER\_CREDIT#) — credit funds on the recipient's account.
- ·Equity (#USER\_EQUITY#) — equity on the recipient's account.
- ·Leverage (#USER\_LEVERAGE#) — account leverage.
- ·Margin (#USER\_MARGIN#) — reserved margin on the recipient's account.
- ·Free margin (#USER\_MARGIN\_FREE#) — free margin on the recipient's account.
- ·Margin level (#USER\_MARGIN\_LEVEL#) — margin level.

The following macros substitute email sending time in the message text:

- ·Trading time — internal platform time.
- ·Local time — time used in the computer on which the platform is installed.
- ·UTC time — [UTC](https://ru.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D0%92%D1%81%D0%B5%D0%BC%D0%B8%D1%80%D0%BD%D0%BE%D0%B5_%D0%BA%D0%BE%D0%BE%D1%80%D0%B4%D0%B8%D0%BD%D0%B8%D1%80%D0%BE%D0%B2%D0%B0%D0%BD%D0%BD%D0%BE%D0%B5_%D0%B2%D1%80%D0%B5%D0%BC%D1%8F)time.

The email sending history is displayed in the Toolbox \ Mailbox tab:

Emails are stored on the trade server. If an email is deleted from the terminal interface, it will not be redownloaded. However, if you delete the terminal email base (the file /bases/server\_name/mail/mailaccount\_number.dat) or connect using another terminal, all the emails for the last 30 days will be downloaded again.

# Attachment directory

To conveniently work with the files that you send by email, create an attachment directory in Manager terminal settings.

| Server:                                    | Broker-Demo   |
|--------------------------------------------|---------------|
| Login:                                     | 1000          |
| Password:                                  | ••••••••      |
| Enable proxy server                        |               |
| Keep personal settings and data at startup |               |
| Hide online users                          |               |
| Enable news                                |               |
| News languages:                            | Auto select   |
| Save files to:                             | D:\nDownloads |
|                                            |               |
|                                            |               |
|                                            |               |
|                                            |               |
|                                            |               |
| Cancel                                     | <b>OK</b>     |
| Help                                       |               |

When you open an attachment, the terminal checks its extension and verifies whether the file contents match this extension. If the file type is allowed and its contents have been successfully validated, the terminal will open the file and save it to the specified directory. Otherwise, a warning will be displayed, notifying that the file may harm the computer, and the file will not be saved. The following file types are allowed: PNG, JPG/JPEG, BMP, ZIP, 7Z, GIF, DOC, XLS, DOCX, XSLX, ODT, RTF, CSV, TXT, LOG.

If a file with the same name and a different content exists in the directory, the manager's login and email arrival date/time up to a second will be added to the saved file name: [login]-[file name]-[date and time].[extension]. For example, the log file 20170501.log will be saved as 1001-20170501-20170502-170038.log.

By default, files are saved to C:\Users\[Windows username]\Downloads\MetaTrader. You can change the default directory by selecting Overview.

<span id="page-69-0"></span>

# Push notifications and SMS

The MetaTrader 5 platform provides fast and efficient tools for sending important information to your traders, for example for sending server operation news or real account opening offers. You can send push notifications or SMS to mobile devices straight from the Manager terminal.

## How to send push notifications

Push notifications are personal messages sent over the Internet, independent of a mobile phone number or carrier. Messages are delivered instantly to MetaTrader 5 mobile terminals. There is no need to keep the application running.

Messages are sent based on MetaQuotes ID, which is a unique user identifier. To obtain this number, the user should install the [MetaTrader 5 mobile terminal](#page-115-0)for iPhone or Android.

Push notifications can be sent directly from Manager terminals.

Select "Push notification / SMS" in the account context menu. An analogous command is available in the account editing dialog. Specify a comma separated list of logins or MetaQuotes IDs and the message text.

You can specify logins as ranges, for example: 1000-2000. Optionally, you can select the required accounts in the list and proceed to email sending. A list of logins for sending will be generated automatically. To enable sending by login number, make sure [MetaQuotes ID](#page-189-0) is specified for the relevant accounts.

Next, enter the message text and click "Send". The maximum message length is limited to 255 characters.

The sending mode affects the automatic signature in push notifications:

- · If you send messages specifying logins, the signature will contain owner company name from the settings of the group to which the accounts belong. Use this type for White Labels, to add the correct company name in the signature.
- ·If MetaQuotes IDs are used, the company name from the Server License will be specified in the signature.

Clients will immediately receive the notifications on their mobile devices. Broker's push notifications appear in the special category of the Messages section in the mobile trading platform:

## Automatic linking of MetaQuotes IDs to accounts

As soon as the trader connects to a trading account, the MetaQuotes ID from their terminal is automatically added to the account on the server side. This works for both desktop and mobile terminals. Thus, the broker will not need to collect this data manually.

#### How to send SMS

Similarly to push notifications, SMS can be sent to traders directly through the Manager terminal.

Select "Push notification / SMS" in the account context menu, then specify logins and enter the SMS text. The message will be sent to the phone number indicated in [account personal data](#page-190-0).

Some SMS providers support the specification of the message sender name. In this case, the receiver will see the company name instead of the phone number. Contact your platform administrator to find out whether this feature is supported by your provider. If it is, fill in the appropriate field when sending an SMS.

The SMS sending option requires a properly configured list of providers available on the trade server. If the function is not available, please contact your platform administrator.

<span id="page-72-0"></span>

# Welcome emails

During account opening, the platform automatically sends to the trader a message via the internal mail system. The email contains the account number and password, as well as general information about the MetaTrader 5 platform.

Customize standard welcome emails by adding your brand logo and information about your services. This is a great place for your promotions: every trader receives the emails, they are displayed in the terminal working area, while the trader receives an audio notification when the email is received.

![](_page_72_Picture_4.jpeg)

The welcome email templates are located in the following directories:

- · **[trade server directory]\templates\greeting\default\\*.htm** — default demo account opening email templates used for all groups.
- · **[trade server directory]\templates\greeting\***custom folder***\\*.htm** — demo account opening email templates, which are sent only to individual account groups in accordance with their settings.
- · **[trade server directory]\templates\greeting\preliminary\\*.htm** — email templates that are sent to the trader when they open a [preliminary account](#page-17-0) from the client terminal.

Template files are named in accordance with the applicable languages. For example, english.htm for English or arabic.htm for Arabic. The sent email language is determined by the client terminal settings.

You can customize these files, but keep in mind the following recommendations:

- · Do not remove information about the login and password of the newly opened account, as this is the only way the traders can receive this data.
- ·You can use [macros](#page-74-0) in emails.
- ·All templates must be in the Unicode encoding (UTF-16/UCS-2 Little Endian).

To activate new email templates, you should restart the main trading server.

The emails are sent regardless of whether the trader opens the account via the client terminal, or the broker opens the account via the Manager or Administrator terminal.

### Different emails for different groups

You can configure different emails for different client groups. This function can be especially useful when working with White Labels, as they will be able to use their own branded messages and assign traders to their managers or support specialists.

Create a separate subdirectory for emails under [server directory]\templates\greeting\. For example, "abcpartner". Move specific White Label templates to this directory.

Next, specify the name of the created directory in the settings of the client groups managed by the White Label.

#### Email macros

You can use macros in emails. They substitute appropriate data depending on the message recipient.

For a complete list of macros please see the following documentation section: [Platform \ Platform components \](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/docs/mt5/platform/components/trade_server/mail_templates) [Trading server \ Email templates](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/docs/mt5/platform/components/trade_server/mail_templates).

<span id="page-75-0"></span>

# MQL5.community chats

Brokerage companies can provide technical support to their clients using MQL5.community chats. The advantage of this method is that chats are built into desktop and mobile terminals. Thus, your traders can receive help while continuing to trade without the need to switch to third-party resources. It is fast and convenient.

[Register an mql5.com account](https://www.mql5.com/en/auth_register) and create [Service Desk](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/servicedesk) ticket to request account binding to your company's servers. A client connecting to any of these servers will see broker's technical support account in the chat contact list.

The broker will see all incoming chats from traders in their MQL5 account and will be able to respond to them:

- ·Via the Messages section on MQL5.com
- · Via a client terminal, provided that the appropriate MQL5 account credentials are specified in terminal [settings](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/docs/mt5/client/settings#community)

The technical support account is always shown in the first place for traders, and it cannot be hidden.

# Company page

Embed a landing page into client terminals to promote your services and to convert potential clients to real traders.

The company page is displayed directly in the Toolbox window, along with other terminal features used by traders on a day-to-day basis:

![](_page_77_Picture_4.jpeg)

Through this page, you can reach your potential clients even without knowing their valid contact details. If a trader opens a demo account on your server, they will see this page.

### Setup

The company page is configured separately for each client group, to ensure flexible customization opportunities:

- ·For real clients, you can display traders rooms with useful functions
- ·For demo accounts, create a landing page to advertise your services
- ·Your White Label partners can show their own advertising pages

Open the Company tab in the group settings and specify a link to your web page in the "Support site" field:
