You can use macros that are replaced on the client terminal side and form the final link:

- · [lang:en|ru|es|..] — the list of language in the format of ISO 639-1. If one of the listed languages matches the interface language of the client terminal, it will be used for the news language. Otherwise, the first language in the list is used.
- ·[login] — client's current login.
- ·[uniq] — the unique ID of the client's computer.

If no address is specified, the default https://www.mql5.com is shown.

<span id="page-79-0"></span>

# Common errors

- · Do not ban demo accounts with missing or incorrect contact information. You can still reach the traders using built-in communication methods: internal mailing system, push messages, welcome emails, and company pages.
- · Specify an internal mailbox for at least one manager account on the server side so that traders can easily contact you.
- · Encourage your clients to use mobile terminals. In addition to offering on-the-go trading capabilities, you will get an additional communication channel.

<span id="page-80-0"></span>

# Reports and analytics

MetaTrader 5 offers a built-in powerful analytics module. Native platform tools provide detailed information on trader activity dynamics, trading account states, commissions, turnovers, and more.

![](_page_80_Figure_2.jpeg)

Finteza integration enables the generation of end-to-end trader analytics to trace trader behavior, from the first website visit to a real account deposit.

In addition to more than 40 standard reports, you can generate any custom reports using the MetaTrader 5 Report API or choose from hundreds of turnkey solutions from third-party developers in the [App Store.](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/market/mt5/reporting)

<span id="page-81-0"></span>

# Server reports

The trading platform features multiple reports reflecting clients' trading activity, working agents, account margin statuses, gateways and much more. All these reports can be accessed from the Server Reports section in the navigator.

![](_page_81_Figure_3.jpeg)

## Requesting a report

The platform automatically requests a report from the server when you select it in the list or change its parameters.

If you want to request reports only manually, disable the "Auto refresh" option. Disabling the option can be useful when working with large reports: by avoiding unnecessary requests, you can reduce the load on the server and Manager terminal.

To manually request data, click in the upper right corner.

Reports can have different settings and filters by groups, dates and symbols. You can change them in the upper right corner of the section.

![](_page_82_Picture_6.jpeg)

Each report has an individual set of available parameters.

- · **Groups** — group of users, on which the report is generated. When making a request, you may specify:
  - ·One of the trading groups or subgroups, for example, demo\\*.
  - ·User logins separated by commas, for example "10011, 10012, 10015".
  - ·Create or use a previously saved [custom set of accounts.](#page-81-0)
- · **Symbols** — the symbol for which the report will be generated (for example, report on operation on a certain symbol). Possibility to filter by symbols depends on the report type.
- · **Period** — custom period for report generation (for example, report on trading operations for the last week). Dates can be specified both manually and using a calendar that is opened when you press . Button allows the selection of one of the predefined time periods to generate a report for.
- · / — switch between two types of reports: regular and dashboard. One or both types can be supported, depending on how the developer implemented the report.

## Requesting reports by client list

You can create and use previously saved account sets for easier requests. Click "Add group of logins" in the context menu of the filter by group.

![](_page_83_Figure_3.jpeg)

Select the necessary account (or multiple accounts using Shift or Ctrl+Shift) and click Add. In order to add all accounts from a client group, select one account with a necessary group and click Group. Similarly, you can add accounts by country.

Specify a name for an account group and click Save. The group will become available for selection in the filter.

You can create a group of accounts based on a csv file (for example, a file received by export in the [Accounts](#page-175-0) or [Online](#page-203-0) section). To do this, click Load and select a file. When importing, a login is taken from the file's first data column beginning from the first line, therefore it does not matter how many columns in total are in the file. The program takes the value up to the nearest separator: a space, a tab, a comma or a semicolon. Sample file:

```
Login;Name;Group;Country;Balance;Credit
1811971;Mitchell Adam;demo\demoforex-5;Morocco;50056.50;0.00
1811972;Richardson Lucas;demo\demoforex-5;Pakistan;49919.47;0.00
1811973;Lewis Landon;demo\demoforex-5;United States;49623.39;0.00
1811974;Cook John;demo\demoforex-5;Ukraine;49900.94;0.00
```

The first line with column names is not imported since a login may only contain numbers.

Account sets are saved separately for each manager account and trading server.

### Report display settings

Using the context menu commands, you can save the report in CSV, Open XML, MHT or HTML file. The context menu of dashboard reports provides diagram settings:

- ·**Title** — show/hide the chart title.
- ·**Legend** — show/hide the chart legend.
- ·**Details** — show/hide the chart details.
- ·**Color** — switch color. The option is used for charts displaying information about an entity.
- · **Type** — switch the chart view: Bar chart, Line chart, Area chart, Donut chart, value (shows the total variable value).
- · **Stacking** — switch chart stacking type. Used for charts, which compare several entities and their contribution to the overall value. For example, you may distribute positions by symbols and market sentiment. Available options:
  - ·**None** — data series are displayed separately
  - ·**With negative values** — data series are combined; values are not summed up
  - ·**Regular** — data series are combined; values are summed up
  - · **100%** — rows are combined, the general contribution of each series to the total value in percentage is shown

### Report cache

Data cache stores data requested from the trading platform database, between report generation times. Use of data cache saves resources when creating reports: the server does not need to re-retrieve data from databases and perform related calculations, while the ready data is already available in the cache.

Over time, the data in the cache can become out of date. For example, if historical data on the server has significantly changed, it is recommended to reset the report cache to ensure proper information display.

To reset the cache, select any report and execute the related command from the report menu. This will reset the cache of all reports, not only the selected one.

![](_page_85_Figure_1.jpeg)

<span id="page-85-0"></span>

#### Standard reports

The standard platform installer includes many ready-made reports:

- ·Accounts Groups — statistical report on accounts in groups on the server.
- ·Accounts Growth — growth in the number of accounts in groups on the server.
- ·Agents — report on all deals related to agent commission operations.
- ·Agents Detailed — extended report on agent commissions.
- ·Credit Facility — report on credit operations for the selected period.
- ·Daily — financial states of requested accounts as at the end of the day.
- ·Daily Detailed — a detailed report on the financial state of one selected account as at the end of the day.
- ·Daily Server Logs — statistical report on platform operation for the selected day.
- ·Daily Dealing — daily report on dealing activity.
- ·Daily Trades — report on trading operations for the selected day.
- ·Daily Orders — report on open orders as at the end of the selected day.
- ·Daily Positions — information about trading positions which remain open at the end of the selected day.
- ·Daily Expert Advisors — trading operations performed by client Expert Advisors.

- ·Deals History — summary report on deals for the selected period.
- ·Deals Profit — detailed report on deals for the selected period.
- ·Deals Initiators — report on deal initiators (reasons).
- ·Deals Geography — trading activity distribution by countries.
- ·Deals Weekly — weekly activity of traders.
- ·Deposit and Withdrawal — deposit and withdrawal operations for the selected period.
- · Equity — financial states of the requested accounts as of the end of a day. The accounts are grouped according to their "Comment" field value. This report allows to generate reports on clients' accounts grouping them by custom criteria.
- ·Margin Calls — report on accounts in the Margin Call or Stop-out state.
- ·Orders History — summary report on orders for the selected period.
- · Segregated — summary report reflecting changes in the financial states of the requested accounts, for the specified period.
- ·Summary — total movements of funds and turnover on the requested accounts for the selected period.
- ·Gateways Turnover — volume of deals processed by gateways.
- · Gateways Profit — volume of deals processed by gateways as well as the broker's profit generated when processing these deals.
- · Gateways White Label — volume of deals executed on the trading server by other brokers via the MetaTrader 5 Gateway (regular or White Label version).
- ·Execution Type — the number, volume and type of executed deals.
- ·Trade Accounts — current trading states of client accounts.
- ·Trade Transactions — detailed information on trading operations executed on MetaTrader 5 servers.
- · Trade Modifications — trading operations that were changed manually by the administrator, by the manager or through the API.
- · StopOut Compensations — operations related to negative account compensations upon Stop-out. It includes all deals with the "so compensation" type.
- ·Money Flow Daily — a group of reports on daily movement of funds on client accounts.
- ·Money Flow Weekly — a group of reports on weekly movement of funds on client accounts.
- ·Lifetime Value — a group of customer lifetime value reports.
- ·First Time Deposit — a group of reports on clients' deposit operations.
- ·Retention of Clients — new customer retention report.

- ·Retention of Trading Accounts — new customer retention report covering separate accounts.
- ·NFA — reports to be submitted to NFA.
- ·EMIR — a report for EMIR regulatory reporting.
- ·Fund Overview — report on key characteristics of an investment fund.
- ·Risk Appetite — report on traders' risk appetite characteristics.

Please see the [MetaTrader 5 Manager documentation](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/docs/mt5/manager/reports)for the complete description of the reports.

The platform administrator determines which reports will be available, when configuring the platform.

#### Configuring reports on the server

Report parameters are managed in the Administrator terminal, because they are generated on the trade server side.

![](_page_87_Picture_10.jpeg)

All reports are displayed in a tree-like list, grouped by the servers for which they are configured. For convenience, you can split them into additional categories: Accounts, Daily Reports, Gateways, etc. Use the context menu of the tree to arrange the reports into subgroups.

Double click on a report to edit its parameters:

- · **Enable** — enable/disable the report. Disabled reports are not available for requests in the Manager terminal.
- ·**Name** — the name of the report configuration.
- · **Type** — this section reflects all reports available on the server. The list is compiled in accordance with the DLL files contained in the /report folder of the main trader server. The platform provides a lot of [standard](#page-85-0) [reports](#page-85-0). You can create additional reports using the MetaTrader 5 Report API or purchase them from the [App Store.](#page-89-0)
- · **Trade server** — to the right of the Type field, select the trade server for which you are configuring the report. The report will use data of the specified server and will only be available when connecting to the specified server.

The bottom of the window features a block of additional report parameters. The list of parameters depends on the specific report. Please see the [MetaTrader 5 Manager documentation](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/docs/mt5/manager/reports)for the description of standard report parameters.

To create a parameter, select Add. String type parameters are created by default. To select another type (integer or fractional), click the arrow on the Add button.

The Information section displays the report description, author, report module version and the version of the MetaTrader 5 Server API using which the report was created.

Also, this section features the data about available report formats (as an HTML page or as a table) along with the required database snapshots. A database snapshot is a state of a certain server database (for example, a database of accounts) registered by the module to create a report.

<span id="page-89-0"></span>

# Additional reports

A wide variety of additional reports is available in the [App Store](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/market/mt5/reporting) on the Support Center website. The App Store showcase is featured directly in Manager terminals, allowing managers to view and order additional products without leaving their workspace:

![](_page_89_Picture_3.jpeg)

Furthermore, you can develop your own reports using [MetaTrader Report API](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/docs/mt5/api/reportapi). The package contains all the necessary tools, alongside the source code examples of standard reports. You can modify the source codes to create your own reports.

<span id="page-90-0"></span>

# Finteza end-to-end analytics

[Finteza](https://www.finteza.com/) is an analytical and advertising system designed to assist brokers in analyzing user behavior on the site and in running marketing campaigns. The system is integrated with MetaTrader 5, and thus the analytics is available straight in the platform: analyze your audience, evaluate the efficiency of marketing campaigns and track the entire trader lifetime, from the first website visit to a real account deposit.

Finteza is integrated with MetaTrader 5, so you can track your traders' actions in terminals. Obtain statistics on which functions and services are the most popular among your traders: algo trading programs, signal copying, account opening and others.

<span id="page-90-1"></span>

#### How to set up analytics

[Order Finteza](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/market/product/402) via the MetaQuotes Support Center. A 3-month trial license is provided for free of charge — test the system in your company and evaluate its efficiency for your business.

[Create an account on the Finteza website](https://www.finteza.com/pricing) and configure tracking on your site (pixel code similar to Facebook or Google pixels) as well as set up the display of ads as HTML5 or raster banners. To set up tracking, copy the code from the Settings \ Counter section of the Finteza panel and insert it into all pages of your website:

Configure required Finteza access permissions to your employees in manager account settings in MetaTrader 5 Administrator. The settings provide special permissions: access to the integration section, access to page views, marketing campaigns and reports.

See also: [Video / Finteza integration with MetaTrader 5](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/articles/956)

#### Server and client events

Finteza automatically accumulates data on your terminal installations and launches. All such events are linked to the website specified in the "Master Site ID" field. These events can be viewed at any time under the Events section of the Finteza panel. This data is only available to you.

Enable tracking of additional trader events via the Integration \ Finteza Analytics section of the Administrator terminal:

- · Demo and preliminary account opening; all events are categorized into actions by unique and non-unique traders (checking if the trader has previously opened an account)
- ·Opening of windows and panels inside the terminal
- ·Launch of indicators and Expert Advisors

- ·Trades: performed automatically by trading robots and manual trades
- ·Usage of MQL5.community services
- ·Usage of Strategy Tester and MetaEditor
- · Server events related to client accounts: live account creation, moving of a preliminary account to a real group and first account deposit

All events, except for the last point, are sent to Finteza by client terminals. When connected to your trading server, the terminals request the relevant settings and sends the appropriate events to the specified tracker. Server events are sent to Finteza by the trading server.

The event naming is formed by the following formula: [Category] + [Subcategory] + [Action].

[Category] + [Subcategory] mean the main source and additional details:

- MetaTrader 5 Trade Server ..... the event occurred on the MetaTrader 5 trading server
- MetaTrader 5 Desktop ..... the event occurred in the MetaTrader 5 desktop version
- MetaTrader 5 iOS ..... the event occurred in MetaTrader 5 mobile for iOS

The main action is indicated as an [Action]:

- ·Expert Add — launching an Expert Advisor on the chart
- ·Account Real Deposit — successful account deposit
- ·Open Editor — opening MetaEditor

So, the events can be as follow:

- · MetaTrader 5 Trade Server Account Real Deposit — funds were deposited in MetaTrader 5 (the event arrived from the trading server)
- ·MetaTrader 5 iOS Account Demo — a demo account was created from the MetaTrader 5 mobile for iOS

To view tracked events, go to the Finteza panel, select your tracker and open the Event report.

To view detailed statistics, enable the option "Add account numbers to reports" via the Administrator terminal. In this case, the relevant account number will be added to the Value field (accordingly, *Account* will be indicated as Units) of each tracked event. If the option is disabled, only your server name will appear as an additional parameter for each event in Finteza.

Finteza automatically associates server and client events with user actions on your site. This action chain provides in-depth end-to-end analytics. View the entire journey of your traders, from their first visit to your website, to opening of a real account, deposits and actions performed in the terminal.

The bottom of the "Finteza Analytics" section features a summary chart on the total number of events sent to your tracker by the trading server and client terminals.

![](_page_94_Figure_5.jpeg)

## Working with affiliates

To manage affiliate programs, enable the "Send account conversion confirmation to affiliates" option under the Integration \ Finteza Analytics section of the Administrator terminal.

An additional utm\_affiliate\_site parameter will be added to all tracked events sent from your terminals to Finteza. Thus, you can track the efficiency of your affiliates in terms of client attraction, using a special Finteza panel section: Sources / Referral.

![](_page_95_Figure_4.jpeg)

## Where do clients come from?

Your platform already has a [client database](#page-6-0) with UTM parameters. This database is created automatically, based on trading accounts on your server.

When a client downloads the terminal installer, Finteza registers information about where the client came from. The relevant details are added to all accounts opened from this terminal. Within the platform, each account is linked to a client record: all account data is copied into the client, including lead source and ad campaign parameters. This generates a client database with information about sources in a fully automatic mode.

Work with the client database via the MetaTrader 5 Manager. For convenience, the Manager terminal provides [filters](#page-177-0) by Lead Campaign and Lead Source. Analyze how many clients you received from a specific ad campaign, as well as evaluate their account statuses and deposits.

#### Analytics in MetaTrader 5 Manager

The Manager terminal provides tools for analyzing broker's audience and traders' preferences, as well as for managing marketing campaigns.

Integration with the [Finteza business analytics system](https://www.finteza.com/) enables the display of information about your site visits, analysis of page efficiency and attraction channels, and other data directly in the terminal.

Information is conveniently presented in the form of charts and diagrams. You can create your own dashboards based on any metrics or use ready presets.

![](_page_99_Figure_1.jpeg)

#### Available metrics

The terminal provides access to various analytical variables. All metrics are divided into groups:

- · **Online Users** — data about users currently connected to the trade server: distribution by geographical location, running balances, software builds, groups, etc.
- · **Clients** — data about all clients and accounts on the trade server: distribution by geographical location, running balances, agents, statuses, marketing campaigns, etc.
- · **Positions** — information about clients' open positions: distribution by symbols, volumes, types, market mood, etc.
- · **Orders** — information about clients' open (active) orders: distribution by symbols, volumes, types, direction, etc.

- · **Finteza Sites** — data on your sites, provided by Finteza: visits, unique users, page views, tracked events, etc. If Finteza metrics are not available, check [settings](#page-90-1) on the platform side and the manager account permissions.
- ·**Finteza Campaigns** — data on your marketing campaigns from Finteza: clicks, CTR, views, etc.
- · **External URL** — browser widget. The widget allows displaying the contents of any web page. For example, you can use it to include your own data in reports.

See also: [Video / Built-in analytics: Boost your sales through efficient advertising](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/articles/947)

#### Predefined sets of metrics

With the predefined sets of metrics, you can quickly get started, without having to create custom dashboards. Open the context menu of the Analytics section and select a preset, which best suits your goals. For example, the "Trading accounts" preset includes diagrams showing the distribution of trading accounts by the registration data, country and group.

![](_page_100_Picture_7.jpeg)

#### Configuring a custom dashboard

You can create your own dashboards using any metrics available. All dashboard settings are stored on the trading server separately for each manager account. Thus, the manager can access dashboards from any computer, on which the Manager terminal is installed.

To create a new dashboard, select Add in the context menu of the Analytics section.

![](_page_101_Picture_4.jpeg)

Then add required metrics using the context menu of the dashboard.

![](_page_102_Figure_1.jpeg)

To change the order of charts, use the context menu or press in the upper right corner. Next, rearrange charts with the mouse.

![](_page_102_Figure_3.jpeg)

In editing mode, you can also change the names of the charts. Click on the current name and enter a new one. To delete a chart, select it and press the Delete key. After making changes, click .

#### Arranging Dashboards

If you are using a large number of dashboards, arrange them in folders, for convenience. For example, you may want to create separate folders for clients and accounts, trading metrics and marketing campaigns.

Click "Add Folder" in the context menu. After creating the necessary folders, drag dashboards into them using the mouse.

![](_page_103_Figure_5.jpeg)

You can create further dashboards directly in the desired folder, using the folder context menu. Nesting is supported, which means you can create subfolders inside the folders.

<span id="page-103-0"></span>

#### Data filters

Data in the charts can be filtered by Lead campaigns and Lead sources, groups and countries, and also by time. For example, you can set the filter to display traders from Germany, who responded to a certain landing page, during the past 5 days.

Filters are located at the top of the dashboard:

![](_page_104_Figure_1.jpeg)

#### Customizing charts

To customize the appearance of a chart, open its context menu and set the desired parameters:

- ·**Title** — show/hide the chart title.
- ·**Legend** — show/hide the chart legend.
- ·**Details** — show/hide the chart details.
- ·**Color** — switch color. The option is used for charts displaying information about an entity.
- · **Type** — switch the chart view: Bar chart, Line chart, Area chart, Donut chart, value (shows the total variable value).
- · **Stacking** — switch chart stacking type. Used for charts, which compare several entities and their contribution to the overall value. For example, you may distribute positions by symbols and market sentiment. Available options:
  - ·**None** — data series are displayed separately
  - ·**With negative values** — data series are combined; values are not summed up
  - ·**Regular** — data series are combined; values are summed up
  - · **100%** — rows are combined; the general contribution of each series to the total value in percentage is shown

· **Top** — set the number of values displayed in the chart. For example, if you set Top = 5, then the chart will display the five largest values.

![](_page_105_Picture_2.jpeg)

<span id="page-106-0"></span>

# Reports for traders

The platform can automatically send trading reports to traders:

- ·Daily reports are sent at the end of each trading day
- ·Monthly reports are sent at the end of each trading month

[At the end of each trading day and month](#page-107-0), the trade server generates client trading report and saves them to [trade server directory]/confirms/YYYYMMDD", where YYYYMMDD is the current date). Each report is an HTML file having the name "login\_mail.htm" (for example, "123\_mail.htm"). The trade server sends the generated reports using the special SendMail utility (its file is stored in the server directory).

The database of all generated reports is saved in the file [trade server directory]/bases/daily.dat. The data from the database can be requested via the Manager terminal, for example, by using a report from [Daily Report](#page-81-0) standard delivery.

## Enabling/disabling daily reports

You can enable or disable reports for individual client groups.

|-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|-----------------------------------|--------|--|------|
|                                                                                                                                                                         |                                   |        |  |      |

You can additionally configure sending of reports to clients and (if necessary) to the technical support by email.

## Configuring the daily report generation time

The time at which daily and monthly report are generated is determined by trade server settings:

| End of day time:       | 23:59                                        |      |
|------------------------|----------------------------------------------|------|
| End of day schedule:   | Monday, Tuesday, Wednesday, Thursday, Friday |      |
| Rollover schedule:     | Monday, Tuesday, Wednesday, Thursday, Friday |      |
| Daily statements:      | End of day                                   |      |
| Last end of day:       | 2021.12.16 23:59:59                          |      |
| Previous end of day:   | 2021.12.15 23:59:59                          |      |
| Monthly statements:    | Last day of month                            |      |
| Last end of month:     | 2021.11.30 23:59:59                          |      |
| Previous end of month: | 2021.10.31 23:59:59                          |      |
| <b>OK</b>              | Cancel                                       | Help |

The reports are affected by the following parameters:

- <span id="page-107-0"></span>·**End of day time** — report generation start time.
- ·**End of day schedule** — report generation days. The option does not affect monthly reports.
- · **Daily statements** — daily report generation time: at the end of a trading day (before charging swaps, annual interest, and commissions) or at the start of a trading day (after charging swaps, annual interest, and commissions).
- · **Monthly statements** — monthly report generation time: at the end of the last day of month (before charging swaps, annual interest, and commissions) or at the start of a first day of the next month (after charging swaps, annual interest, and commissions).

#### Report Data

| Issued to         | Description                                                                                              |
|-------------------|----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| Datetime          | Daily report generation date and time.                                                                   |
| Login             | Initial key. Client login for which the daily report is generated.                                       |
| Issued to         | Description                                                                                              |
| DatetimePrev      | Previous daily report generation date and time.                                                          |
| Name              | The name of the client in the daily report.                                                              |
| Group             | The group of the client in the daily report.                                                             |
| Currency          | The currency of the client's deposit in the daily report.                                                |
| Company           | The company serving the client in the daily report.                                                      |
| EMail             | The email of the client in the daily report.                                                             |
| Balance           | The client's balance in the daily report.                                                                |
| Credit            | The client's credit funds in the daily report.                                                           |
| InterestRate      | The annual interest rate for the client in the daily report.                                             |
| CommissionDaily   | Commission fees charged from the client for the day in the report.                                       |
| CommissionMonthly | Commission fees charged from the client for the current month in the report.                             |
| AgentDaily        | Agent commissions charged for the client's trading operations for the day.                               |
| AgentMonthly      | Agent commissions charged for the client's trading operations for the current month.                     |
| BalancePrevDay    | The client's balance at the end of the previous day.                                                     |
| BalancePrevMonth  | The client's balance at the end of the previous trading month.                                           |
| EquityPrevDay     | The client's equity at the end of the previous day.                                                      |
| EquityPrevMonth   | The client's equity at the end of the previous trading month.                                            |
| Margin            | The client's margin in the daily report.                                                                 |
| MarginFree        | The client's free margin in the daily report.                                                            |
| MarginLevel       | The client's margin level in the daily report.                                                           |
| MarginLeverage    | The client's margin leverage in the daily report.                                                        |
| Profit            | Current profit for all open positions of the client in the daily report.                                 |
| ProfitStorage     | Current swaps charged for the client's open positions for the day, but not yet reflected in the balance. |
| ProfitEquity      | The client's current floating equity in the daily report.                                                |
| DailyProfit       | The client's recorded daily profit.                                                                      |
| DailyBalance      | The amount added to the client's balance during the reported day.                                        |
| Issued to         | Description                                                                                              |
| DailyCredit       | Credit given to the client during the reported day.                                                      |
| DailyCharge       | Other charges to the client's balance during the reported day.                                           |
| DailyCorrection   | Corrective balance operations for the reported day.                                                      |
| DailyBonus        | Bonuses added to the client's balance for the reported day.                                              |
| DailyStorage      | Swaps charged to the client for the reported day.                                                        |
| DailyCommInstant  | Instant commissions charged for the reported day.                                                        |
| DailyCommRound    | Turnover commissions charged for the reported day.                                                       |
| DailyCommFee      | Fees charged for the client's deals for the reported day.                                                |
| DailyAgent        | Agent commissions charged for a client's trade operations for the reported day.                          |
| DailyInterest     | The amount accrued to the client as part of the annual interest rate for the reported day.               |

### Customizing report appearance

Email templates for daily and monthly reports are located in the trade server directory:

- · **[server directory]\templates\confirmation\default\\*.htm** — default templates of daily reports used for all groups.
- · **[server directory]\templates\confirmation\custom folder\\*.htm** — daily report templates that are only sent to separate groups in accordance with their settings.
- · **[server directory]\templates\statement\default\\*.htm** — default templates of monthly reports used for all groups.
- · **[server directory]\templates\statement\custom folder\\*.htm** — templates of monthly reports that are only sent to separate groups in accordance with their settings.

Template files are named in accordance with the applicable languages. For example, english.htm for English or arabic.htm for Arabic. The sent email language is determined by the client terminal settings.

You can customize these files, but keep in mind the following recommendations:

- ·All templates must be in the Unicode encoding (UTF-16/UCS-2 Little Endian).
- ·You can use [macros](#page-110-0) in templates.

To activate a new email template, you should restart the main trading server.

### Separate reports for groups

You can configure different emails for different client groups. This function can be especially useful when working with White Label partners, as they will be able to use their own branded messages and contact details.

Create a separate subdirectory for templates in [server directory]\templates\confirmation\. For example, "abcpartner". Move specific White Label report templates to this directory.

Next, specify the name of the created directory in the settings of the client groups managed by the White Label.

| Group            |                                                                   |
|------------------|-------------------------------------------------------------------|
| Common           |                                                                   |
| Company          | ABC Partner                                                       |
| Company site     | abcbroker.com                                                     |
| Company email    | contact@abcbroker.com                                             |
| Deposit site     | abcbroker.com/deposit                                             |
| Withdrawal site  | abcbroker.com/withdraw                                            |
| Support site     | https://www.abcbroker.com/[lang:en ru es pt zh]                   |
| Support email    | support@abcbroker.com                                             |
| Templates folder | abcpartner                                                        |
|                  |                                                                   |
|                  | <button>OK</button> <button>Cancel</button> <button>Help</button> |

Restart the platform to apply the new templates.

<span id="page-110-0"></span>

### Macros for reports

You can use macros in reports. They substitute appropriate data depending on the message recipient.

For a complete list of macros please see the following documentation section: [Platform \ Platform components \](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/docs/mt5/platform/components/trade_server/mail_templates) [Trading server \ Email templates](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/docs/mt5/platform/components/trade_server/mail_templates).

# Exporting client accounts

You can export the account database as file and analyze it in third-party applications.

Open the relevant section in MetaTrader 5 Manager and select Export in the context menu. After that, specify the directory where the file will be saved, as well as export settings.

![](_page_111_Picture_4.jpeg)

In the "Save as type" field, select the destination file format: \*.HTML or \*.CSV. Next, select the export option: The following export variants are available here:

·**All** — export all information, regardless of the currently selected columns.

- ·**Visible** — export only those columns that are currently enabled.
- ·**Selected** — open the below described window to manually specify columns for export.

# Common errors

- · If a report is not available to you, check whether the report is configured on the server and whether your manager account has the necessary rights.
- · If the report does not contain the required clients, check whether your manager account has access to the relevant groups on the server.
- · If you receive no data when requesting any daily report (beginning with "Daily"), check if the generation of daily reports is enabled in the client group settings.
- · If the Analytics section does not feature metrics on advertising campaigns, make sure that you have a Finteza license and that your manager account has the necessary permissions.
- · A Finteza license is required to enable automated filling of "Lead source" and "Lead campaign" fields in accounts opened via the terminals.

<span id="page-114-0"></span>

# Attracting new clients

The success of a brokerage company primarily depends on the number of clients and their trading activity. The more clients you attract, the more trades they perform, the stronger your business becomes. On the other hand, traders are constantly looking for better deals and for more favorable trading conditions. That is why it is important to provide the best possible offerings in order to prevent clients from leaving for your competitors.

The MetaTrader 5 platform provides a plethora of ready-made solutions for attracting new clients:

- ·Mobile and web terminals for comfortable trading from anywhere in the world
- ·Deep links for highly efficient trader rooms
- ·Lead purchasing program that redirects traders to your servers
- ·Advertising on trading resources
- ·Business expansion capabilities through White Label partners
- ·Additional trading services to assist beginners
- ·Ability to offer sponsored hosting to increase customer loyalty and to increase trading volumes

The platform provides extra capabilities to evaluate the effectiveness of customer acquisition strategies. Native integration with the Finteza analytics and the built-in client management system enable the evaluation of various channels. Access statistics on how many people use mobile terminals, how purchased leads convert to real customers and how much they pay.

Use every opportunity to attract new users and to retain existing customers.

<span id="page-115-0"></span>

# MetaTrader 5 mobile terminals for iOS and

Mobile trading in MetaTrader 5 provides the ability to connect to your platform and to trade anytime and from anywhere in the world. With a smartphone or tablet with the MetaTrader 5 app for iOS or Android, your traders can access financial information, control their trading accounts and trade 24 hours a day.

Mobile trading enhances the overall user experience. Furthermore, in addition to building customer loyalty, mobile platforms can increase the broker's trading turnover by providing easy access to trading capabilities.

![](_page_115_Figure_4.jpeg)

With [push notifications](#page-69-0) available on mobile devices, you will receive an additional communication channel. You will be able to reach your potential clients without even having their contact details.

Built-in [chats](#page-75-0) allow traders to communicate with each other, as well as provide an additional tool for broker's technical support. Traders can contact your support team directly and receive responses straight on their mobile device.

Due to all these exciting opportunities, mobile terminals can be considered an indispensable part of any brokerage business.

#### See also:

- ·[Documentation / MetaTrader 5 iPhone/iPad](https://www.metatrader5.com/en/mobile-trading/iphone/help)
- ·[Documentation / MetaTrader 5 Android](https://www.metatrader5.com/en/mobile-trading/android/help)

#### How to order a mobile terminal

Unlike the desktop platform, mobile terminals allow the connection only to those servers which are present in the list built into the application. If your company has no mobile terminal White Label, your trading platform will not be included into the list of servers, and traders will not be able to connect to it.

The general list of servers in all mobile terminals is updated automatically if 24 hours have passed since the last launch of the terminal:

- · After ordering a mobile terminal for one of the White Labels (as a separate component or as part of a package), the relevant WL's servers are added to the general list.
- · If a decision is made to cancel the mobile terminal of one of the White Labels, the relevant servers will be removed from the general list.

The mobile terminal White Label can be ordered via the [App Store](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/market/mt5?Text=mobile&Category=&vendor=1) section of the Support Center website.

You can order mobile terminals not only for your company, but also for your White Label. Your affiliates will be able to attract traders to their servers and maintain their own analytics.

See also: [Articles / White Label for MetaTrader 5](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/articles/252#mobile_terminal_wl)

#### Use proper download links

After you order a mobile White Label, your servers will be added to the list of available servers. Any trader will be able to download the mobile terminal from the relevant app store and connect to your server.

However, for maximum efficiency, we recommend that you use special download links for mobile terminals. Such links are available under the [White Label](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/market/whitelabel/mt5) section of the Support Center website.

```
https://download.mql5.com/cdn/mobile/mt5/ios?server=[list-of-your-servers]
https://download.mql5.com/cdn/mobile/mt5/android?server=[list-of-your-servers]
```

These links already feature your company's servers. When users download a mobile terminal from such a link, your platform will be immediately shown in the list of servers in the account opening/connection window. This is especially important for attracting new clients, as they are likely to request a demo account on your server.

You can also add UTM parameters to these links to track the efficiency of your client attraction strategies through mobile applications.

```
https://download.mql5.com/cdn/mobile/mt5/ios?utm_campaign=[your-campaign-id]&
server=[list-of-your-servers]
https://download.mql5.com/cdn/mobile/mt5/android?utm_campaign=[your-campaign-id]&
server=[list-of-your-servers]
```

The specified parameters will be automatically registered in the Lead Source and Lead Campaign fields of all trading accounts opened through terminals downloaded at such links. Based on UTM parameters, you can select specific accounts, filter [server reports](#page-81-0) and [analytical dashboards.](#page-103-0) All these details enable in-depth analytics on attracted traders, such as their country, trading activity, deposits, etc.

Lead Source and Lead Campaign data from terminal download links is registered in created accounts only if you have a [Finteza license](#page-90-0).

### Platform-side configuration

Make sure to properly configure account groups in your platform for the correct operation of White Labels and mobile terminals.

When registering a new WL, do not forget to add new or edit existing account groups to work under the label: simply select the necessary company name in the Company field of the group settings in MetaTrader 5 Administrator:

| Group:            | demo\forex-usd                                 |
|-------------------|------------------------------------------------|
|                   |                                                |
| Company           | Forex Broker                                   |
| Company site:     | forexbroker.com                                |
| Company email:    | mail@forexbroker.com                           |
| Deposit site:     | forexbroker.com/deposit                        |
| Withdrawal site:  | forexbroker.com/withdraw                       |
| Support site:     | forexbroker.com/[lang:en es pt ru]/landing-mt5 |
| Support email:    | support@forexbroker.com                        |
| Templates folder: |                                                |
|                   |                                                |
|                   | <b>OK</b> Cancel <b>Help</b>                   |

If no company name or an incorrect company name is specified in the account group settings, your traders will see the "invalid server name" error in mobile terminals and will not be able to connect to your platform:

The mobile terminal journal will feature the following logs:

You were trying to connect to ForexBroker-Server but have connected to a different server.

To fix the error, select a valid company name in the group settings and ask the trader to re-connect to your platform.

The exact company name is available in your White Label request, in the list of [White Labels](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/market/whitelabel/mt5) and in the relevant White Label [editing from](#page-146-0).

<span id="page-119-0"></span>

# Deep Linking in MetaTrader mobile terminals

Advanced deep linking technologies enable the redirection of users to predefined sections of the mobile application. The technology provides extensive opportunities in trading, such as navigation to the required account from the registration email or jumping to a trading operation in MetaTrader after receiving a signal in your analytical application.

# Website integration: increase conversions and improve customer experience

You can improve the mobile versions of your websites, make them more user-friendly and functional. Add links to the trader's room to switch to a mobile terminal for each account. By selecting the link, the client will switch to MetaTrader with the specified account and server specified automatically. The user will only need to enter the password. You can also add direct links to demo account opening in the mobile application — your potential customer can try trading in just a couple of clicks.

Another extremely important feature is deep-linking in registration emails. Add links to the emails that you send to customers upon account registration, and the users will not need to specify the account number and the server name manually. The relevant details will be substituted automatically, while the trader will only need to enter the password.

![](_page_119_Picture_6.jpeg)

The fewer steps on the way to opening an account, the greater the chance that your potential customer will convert into a real one, and the real customer will stay longer with you.

# Integration with a mobile app: trading function to complement your analytics

Very few of top finance applications on Google Play and App Store offer trading features, while existing trading applications provide very limited functionality.

It is practically impossible to reach the performance and reliability offered by native applications even by using server plug-ins for integration. Many companies focus on providing pure analytics: quotes, technical and fundamental analysis, trading advice and news.

The capabilities of such applications can also be expanded through deep links that lead to MetaTrader. For example, if your application generates EURUSD buy signals, you can create a link that opens the Trade section of MetaTrader, in which the desired symbol is already selected. The trader will only need to specify volume and tap "Buy".

![](_page_120_Figure_6.jpeg)

## How it works

Selection of a special hyperlink located on your website or in your application, opens the MetaTrader mobile app platform and a desired section in it. The link should start with metatrader5://... . The part of link before "://" is

called [a URL scheme.](https://ru.wikipedia.org/wiki/URL) Popular schemes are http, ftp, mailto etc. During installation, MetaTrader 5 terminals register the metatrader5 URL scheme in the operating system to enable the use of appropriate links on this device.

The general scheme of links:

metatrader5://path?par1=val1&...&parN=valN

Where:

- ·'path' is the destination section of the application
- ·'par' is the list of parameters

#### Connection to a specified account on the server

The link should contain the application section, to which you want to link your traders, as well as the 'login' and 'server' parameters containing the account number and the name of the server to connect to. Example:

metatrader5://account?login=123456&server=MetaQuotes-Demo

Upon a click on such a link, the MetaTrader 5 app starts and tries to connect to the specified account. If the account is already present in the terminal and has a predefined password, connection is performed successfully. Otherwise, the account connection form is shown with the account and server already specified, and a user is prompted to enter the password. A server name should exactly match the name in the White Label.

- · If the server name is not specified, the server of the current account or of the first account from the list will be used.
- · If the account number is not specified, and the server name is specified, any account from the specified server will be selected. If there are no such accounts, the scheme will not be executed.
- · If both the account and the server are not specified, the current account or the first account from the list will be used.

#### Link to a specified section

In order to forward users to the specified section, specify the appropriate 'path' value in the link:

```
metatrader5://<path>?login=123456&server=MetaQuotes-Demo
```

After <path>, you can specify the account number and the server name, to which the terminal should connect before opening the section. If the account or server is not specified, the connection rules described above apply.

- ·account — no specific section will be opened; it is used for connection to a specified account and server.
- · marketwatch — quotes. The example contains the additional 'symbols' parameter, in which financial instruments to be displayed in the window are specified.

metatrader5://marketwatch?server=MetaQuotes-Demo&symbols=USDRUB,XAUUSD,%23DIS,%23IBM

·chart/symbol/period is a chart with the specified symbol and timeframe, e.g.:

metatrader5://chart/XAUUSD/H1?server=MetaQuotes-Demo

·trade — Trade tab, the list of open positions and orders, e.g.:

metatrader5://trade?server=MetaQuotes-Demo

· trade/symbol — a trade dialog, from which a trading operation on the specified symbol can be performed, e.g.:

metatrader5://trade/SILVER?server=MetaQuotes-Demo&login=1145990

Illegal characters in URI links must be encoded. For example, the #GOOG must be specified as % 23GOOG. Names of financial instruments are case sensitive.

Some email services may erase links beginning with "metatrader4://" or "metatrader5://". In this case, instead of deep link, insert a link to a certain section of your website, which in turn re-directs users to the mobile application.

#### Additional parameters

It is possible to specify additional parameters for each deep linked section:

- ·'login' and 'server' can be used for connection with the specified account number and server name.
- · symbols — one or more symbols separated by commas, which should be added to the Quotes section after connection to an account defined by the 'login' and 'server' parameters. Illegal characters in URI links must be encoded. For example, the #GOOG must be specified as %23GOOG. Names of financial instruments are case sensitive. The indicated symbols do not replace those already existing in the Quotes section, while they are added to the current list.

<span id="page-123-0"></span>

# MetaQuotes Leads

MetaTrader 5 Web Terminal allows users to trade in financial markets from any web browser. It works in all operating systems and browsers, while requiring no extra software installations. The application is safe to use: the transmitted data is securely encrypted.

The Web Terminal allows placing all types of market and pending orders, as well as supports one-click trading. Traders can view real-time quotes and analyze charts using basic graphical objects and thirty technical indicators.

The terminal is a modern HTML5 application that can be easily integrated into any website via a simple iframe widget.

![](_page_123_Figure_5.jpeg)

The web terminal is located entirely on the broker's access servers. It operates as an individual web terminal, which only works with your platform. This provides maximum security and full control for the broker.

When connecting to the web terminal via your website, the trader should only enter the login and password, without having to select a server. The platform will determine which server (demo, real, main or additional trading server) the account belongs to. For the seamless web terminal integration with the clients area on the broker website, the user login can be pre-selected to let your client enter a password only. If a user saves a password in a browser storage, the account will be connected automatically on the next run.

## Web Terminal benefits for brokers

The web terminal provides an opportunity to attract new clients by lowering the entry threshold. The web terminal provides fewer functions, and yet it offers everything a trader may need to get started. For example, there are no algorithmic trading features, Market or Signals. It will be easier for the trader to understand the main terminal features.

Also, the web terminal provides complete device independence. The terminal can be accessed from any device powered by Windows or macOS, as well tablet or phone. The user will only need a web browser to manage the account. Your clients can trade from anywhere, and thus you can generate additional profits from increased trade volumes.

To obtain the terminal, please [order it via the App Store section](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/market/product/248)of the Support website.

## How to set up the platform

The web terminal operates on an [access server](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/docs/mt5/platform/components/access_server) which acts as a web server.

Register a domain (or a subdomain for your existing domain) where the web terminal will run. Below we use the domain webtrading.broker.com as an example — you should replace it with your own domain.

Associate your domain with the [public IP address](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/docs/mt5/platform/administration/admin_network/network_add_edit/network_access_server#public) of your access server. For this purpose, the appropriate record must be specified on the DNS hosting. For example, for webtrading.broker.com host, the following entry should be registered in DNS: "XXX.XXX.XXX.XXX webtrading.broker.com", where XXX.XXX.XXX.XXX is the public address of the access server.

Next, open Integration \ Web services in MetaTrader 5 Administrator and add an SSL certificate for the domain. The certificate will enable connections to the access server using the HTTPS protocol.

You can add extra certificates for your White Label partners to provide a separate web terminal for each required company's domain. After that, in the relevant field, specify which company the domain belongs to.

It is recommended to have port 443 available on the server for HTTPS connections. In this case, your clients will not need to explicitly specify the port in the address bar to open the web terminal page.

No other settings are needed. Your web terminal will be available at https://webtrading.broker.com/terminal/.

See also: [Documentation / MetaTrader 5 Administrator / Integration / Web Services](https://support.metaquotes.net/ru/docs/mt5/platform/administration/integration/integration_web_services)

#### If you have multiple access servers

For each platform we recommend using at least two access servers: for the main and backup configuration. As your customer database grows and your business geography expands, the number of servers will increase to ensure high-quality service.

To enable connections to your web terminal via any of your access servers, add A-records with their public addresses to the DNS server. Thus, you will have several records with different addresses but with the same domain:

```
XXX.XXX.XXX.XXX webtrading.broker.com
YYY.YYY.YYY.YYY webtrading.broker.com
ZZZ.ZZZ.ZZZ.ZZZ webtrading.broker.com
```

XXX, YYY and ZZZ are the addresses of your access servers. There can be any number of them.

No additional actions are required on the platform side. The domain certificate uploaded via the Integration \ Web Services section is used for all access servers.

To balance the load by distributing connections to the web terminal between access servers, use GeoDNS or GLSB services. Please check with your hosting provider whether this service is available.

#### How to add the web terminal to your site

To install the web terminal on your site, simply place the <iframe> tag on the desired page. The address of your web terminal should be specified as the source in this tag:

```
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <title>Web Terminal</title>
</head>
<body>
  <iframe src="https://webtrading.broker.com/terminal?
mode=connect&marketwatch=EURUSD,GBPUSD,USDJPY&utm_campaign=webterminal&utm_source=site"
width="100%" height="1000"></iframe>
</body>
</html>
```

Additional web terminal settings can be specified as URL parameters:

```
<iframe src="https://webtrading.broker.com/terminal?
parameter1=value1&parameter2=value2"></iframe>
```

The following parameters are supported:

**first\_name, second\_name, email** — first name, last name and email to be inserted into the demo and real account registration form. By using these parameters, you can make account opening easier for traders who have already registered on your site and provided the required data. Example:

```
https://webtrading.broker.com/terminal?
mode=demo&first_name=John&second_name=Smith&email=johnsmith%40mail.com
```

Please note that the example includes the "mode=demo" parameter to automatically open the demo account registration form upon the web terminal launch.

<span id="page-126-0"></span>**utm\_campaign, utm\_source** — utm parameters that will be added to the [relevant fields of accounts](#page-191-0) opened via the web terminal. Using them, you can analyze how efficiently you attract traders via web terminals. For further details please see ["How to track accounts opened via the web terminal".](#page-128-0) Example:

https://webtrading.broker.com/terminal?utm\_campaign=webterminal&utm\_source=site

The parameters are used only at the first start. After that the web terminal will use the user-specified data.

**mode** — the web terminal page that opens on first launch. Supported values:

- ·demo — demo account registration form
- ·real — real account registration form
- ·connect — existing account connection form

https://webtrading.broker.com/terminal?mode=connect

The parameter is used only if the user has not previously connected to the account. If the web terminal has a saved account, it will be connected immediately upon startup.

**login** — trading account for connection. If this parameter is specified, the web terminal will be launched with a pre-filled account connection dialog. The user will only have to enter a password. Use this parameter to create convenient client areas.

https://webtrading.broker.com/terminal?login=123456

If an account is specified in the URL, no demo account will be crated at the first launch (the option is managed via [platform settings\)](#page-9-0). This also prevents from connecting using a previously saved account.

**marketwatch** — the list of symbols to be displayed by default in Market Watch is specified by the marketwatch parameter. Example:

https://webtrading.broker.com/terminal?marketwatch=EURUSD,GBPUSD,AUDCAD,USDJPY

The parameter also determines the order in which the symbols are displayed. In the example above, on the first web terminal launch the user will see only four specified symbols in the Market Watch window, EURUSD will be the first and USDJPY will be the last. Later the user can re-configure the list of symbols, and the new settings will be saved in the browser. The maximum number of symbols in the parameter is 300.

**theme-mode** — theme for the application interface. Possible values:

- ·0 — light (default)
- ·1 — dark

https://webtrading.broker.com/terminal?theme-mode=1

The parameter is only used for the first start. After that the web terminal will use user-specified settings. If necessary, you can force a user-defined theme to be overridden. This may be useful, for example, if your site supports light and dark themes. This will allow the synchronization of interface switching modes of the site and the web terminal.

To switch the color scheme of the web terminal, call postMessage on the <iframe> element, through which the web terminal is loaded. Specify the parameters type: 'mt5\_update' and theme-mode: 0 (for the light theme) or 1 (for the dark theme):

```
const iframe = document.getElementById('iframe');
iframe.contentWindow.postMessage({
  type: 'mt5_update',
  'theme-mode': 1,
}, '*');
```

**theme** — color scheme for the application interface. Possible values:

- ·greenRed — red and green (default)
- ·blueRed — blue and red
- ·blackWhite — black and white
- ·neutral — neutral

https://webtrading.broker.com/terminal?theme=blueRed

The parameter is used only at the first start. After that, the web terminal will apply user-specified settings.

**lang** — web terminal interface language. The following languages are currently supported:

· English (en), Arabic (ar), Bulgarian (bg), Czech (cs), Chinese Simplified (zh), Chinese Traditional (zt), Hebrew (he), Hungarian (hu), Dutch (nl), French (fr) ), German (de), Greek (el), Hindi (hi),

· Indonesian (id), Italian (it), Japanese (ja), Korean (ko), Malay (ms), Persian (fa), Polish (pl), Portuguese (pt), Romanian (ro), Russian (ru), Spanish (es), Thai (th), Turkish (tr), Ukrainian (uk), Uzbek (uz), Vietnamese (vi)

https://webtrading.broker.com/terminal?lang=de

The parameter is used only at the first start. After that, the web terminal will apply user-specified settings.

The parameter is only used for the first start. Then the web terminal will apply user-specified settings.

The size of the web terminal window on the page can be set using standard "width" and "height" parameters in the <iframe> tag, for example:

```
<iframe src="https://webtrading.broker.com/terminal" width="100%"
height="1000"></iframe>
```

Generally we recommended to set the width and height to 100% so that the web terminal block takes up all the available web page space. If you specify an absolute size in pixels, some elements may be out of the visible window on some devices.

#### How to set up the account opening form

To configure the account opening form, use MetaTrader 5 Administrator. Go to the Clients & Account \ Allocations section and set available account types, leverages and balance.

#### Security settings

Prevent the web terminal page from loading in IFRAME. An attacker can place an invisible IFRAME containing the page of your website with the web terminal and combine the web terminal control element (such as a button) with another link on their website. Thus, when clicking a link, a user may actually perform an action necessary to the attacker.

Add [X-Frame-Options: DENY](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTTP/X-Frame-Options) HTTP header to your page containing the web terminal in order to disable page loading in IFRAME and protect users.

Only use the web terminal widget on https pages. The page on which the widget is installed must work over a secure https protocol (not http). Otherwise, the operation of the web terminal will not be possible in some browsers (for example Chrome version 60 and higher).

<span id="page-128-0"></span>

#### How to track accounts opened via the web terminal

Special UTM parameters are added to all accounts opened via the web terminal. Such UTM parameters inform the broker that the potential client has come from a web terminal operating on the broker's website. The UTM tags are added to the [trading account parameters](#page-191-0):

· The Comment field will contain "WebTerminal [short name of the domain from which the account was opened]". Example: "WebTerminal mysite.com". The "www" part is removed from the address.

- · The domain name with 'www' is also added in the 'Lead source' field. Example: "www.mysite.com". The value can be overridden by adding utm\_source to the widget parameters.
- · The 'Lead campaign' field is not filled by default. You may add utm\_campaign to widget parameters in order to write the name of your marketing campaign to this field.

To use your own UTM parameters for tracking clients, add the [utm\\_source and utm\\_campaign parameters](#page-126-0) to the web terminal widget.

| Name          | John Smith        |
|---------------|-------------------|
| Company       | Individual        |
| Language      | English           |
| MetaQuotes ID | 56DC7613          |
| Lead campaign | web.demo          |
| Lead source   | www.abcbroker.com |
| Email         | john@smith.com    |
| Phone         | +12251325673      |
| Country       | United States     |
| State         | New York          |
| City          | New York          |
| Zip code      | 4032              |
| Address       | 24 Wall st.       |
| Comment       | abcbroker.com     |

For the widget added to the www.abcbroker.com site, account parameters will be filled as follows:

- ·Comment = WebTerminal
- ·Lead source = www.abcbroker.com
- ·Lead campaign = web.demo

#### Mobile version of the web platform

The MetaTrader 5 web platform includes a special version adapted for iOS and Android smartphones and tablets. This enables convenient trading from mobile browsers in addition to desktop ones.

No additional settings for the mobile version are needed. The web terminal will automatically detect the user's device by the browser's "user-agent" and will adapt the interface.

![](_page_130_Figure_2.jpeg)

You can also embed a web terminal in your mobile app. To do this, create a web form in it with the web terminal <iframe>.

#### Minimum browser versions

The web terminal supports the following web browser versions and above:

·Microsoft Edge 15

- ·Mozilla Firefox 54
- ·Google Chrome 51
- ·Safari 12
- ·Opera 38

#### Troubleshooting

If a trader receives a 404 error when trying to open the web terminal page, check the following:

- ·The SSL certificate you uploaded in the Integration \ Web Service section is valid and has not expired
- · Your company name is specified correctly in the SSL certificate — it must match the company name specified in the license/White Label
- ·Your platform does not run under a test license — in this case the web terminal is not available
- ·The web terminal is included in your platform license. Contact the [support team](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/support) to check the license

# MetaQuotes Leads

Brokerage companies have a unique opportunity to purchase leads with verified contact details for the MetaTrader 5 trading platform. You order leads from preferred countries and start registering new accounts on your demo server. Instead of searching for customers, you can obtain registered users with real contact information. Connect your sales team and convert such leads into paying customers.

The lead generation program is based on targeting options in [the Finteza Analytics system.](https://www.finteza.com/) When the user downloads MetaTrader 5 form MetaQuotes' corporate resources, Finteza analyzes user parameters. Users from target countries will be automatically offered to open a demo account on your trading server. Furthermore, the trader's contact details are validated during registration.

Each completed account opening event is tracked in Finteza. You only pay if a user has opened a demo account and an appropriate event has been confirmed in the tracker. Potential client data with complete contact details instantly appear in your MetaTrader 5 platform. The information is available in Manager and Administrator terminals and can further be exported to any CRM or BI system for further analysis.

![](_page_133_Picture_5.jpeg)

# Lead generation process

1. The user downloads the client terminal from MetaQuotes' corporate websites (metaquotes.net, metatrade4.com, metatrade5.com, mql4.com and mql5.com) or from advertising sources.

Terminals downloaded from brokers' websites can only connect to the relevant broker's servers registered in WL and licenses. Such downloads do not participate in the program.

2. The user installs and launches the downloaded terminal for the first time.

3. Finteza checks targeting options. If the user falls under the specified criteria, such as the target country, then only your servers will be displayed in the connection list in the trading terminal.

4. The user is automatically prompted to open a demo account on your server.

Pay attention that analytics only cover open accounts. If the user does not complete the required actions, the event will not be registered in the analytical system.

To start receiving verified contact details of traders during account registration, set up [email and phone](#page-29-0) [confirmation](#page-29-0). This is a key step towards efficient potential customer management.

5. Information about the potential client will be added directly to your MetaTrader 5 and to your Finteza tracker. The events related to new demo accounts registered on your server are confirmed in the tracker. These events can also be monitored from the MetaTrader 5 Manager or Administrator terminal.

Special UTM parameters are used for tracking:

- ·utm\_source=metaquotes
- ·utm\_campaign=install.metaquotes

6. Your sales managers can start working with these potential clients in an effort to convert them into real accounts. A client can be contacted using [built-in communication channels:](#page-63-0) email, push notifications, SMS and others.

![](_page_135_Figure_1.jpeg)

# Configure onboarding functions in MetaTrader 5

First, you should enable verification of contact details, opening of [demo accounts](#page-7-0) and request of [real accounts](#page-17-0) straight from the terminal.

MetaTrader 5 provides the ability to [validate phone numbers and emails](#page-29-0) as specified by users when opening accounts from client terminals. The option is implemented through the integration of email and SMS services. If you do not enable the contact verification option, traders can indicate invalid phone numbers and emails during registration.

## **Tracking and end-to-end analytics in Finteza and in MetaTrader 5**

The entire client conversion path can be tracked in Finteza. This applies to any customer acquisition channels, including MetaQuotes Leads and all other traffic sources.

Available features:

- · End-to-end analytics in client terminals and websites, deposit tracking and customer lifetime value reports (LTV) by traffic sources.
- · Trader profiling enriching client records. Access to extended information increases the efficiency of your client acquisition and retention strategies and also assists in saving resources at the sales stage.
- · Conversion funnels can be used to determine efficient client attraction channels, as well as to find new ROMI and conversion rate boosters.
- · A two-stage bot traffic detection system can reduce advertising budget costs. Advertising partners may deliver bot traffic if you do not control the traffic quality. Finteza provides efficient and reliable quality control tools.
- · Website loading speed reports by country and device. Measure your website loading speed in target countries. If a page loads for more than 3 seconds, you are definitely losing conversions along the entire process, from registration to deposits. This leads to inefficient budgeting of your customer attraction strategies.
- ·An advertising engine with a retargeting option based on site visits and user actions in the platform.

All analytical reports feature full unsampled data. You always access complete real-time information about user actions.

Finteza is the only system which can analyze user behavior in MetaTrader terminals:

![](_page_138_Figure_12.jpeg)

By default, the Finteza panel displays data on terminal installations and launches. You can additionally enable an expanded list of events:

- ·Demo and preliminary account opening
- ·Opening of windows and panels inside the terminal
- ·Launch of indicators and Expert Advisors
- ·Trades performed automatically by trading robots and manual trades

- ·Usage of [MQL5.community](https://www.mql5.com/) services
- ·Usage of Strategy Tester and MetaEditor
- · Server events related to client accounts: live account creation, moving of a preliminary account to a real group and first account deposit

[Enabled advanced analytics](#page-90-1) under the "Integration \ Finteza Analytics" section in MetaTrader 5 Administrator.

## **Benefits of MetaQuotes Leads**

- ·The program drives targeted users with validated contact details.
- · The targeting option allows the selection of preferred countries or allows a limitation in the number of accounts from specific countries.
- · You receive full control over the client acquisition process. Relevant statistics are available in the Finteza panel and in the MetaTrader 5 Manager and Administrator terminals.
- · You can boost the efficiency of your lead management strategies and deliver useful information using onboarding and contact options directly in the platform.

You can order leads in the [App Store](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/market/product/550) section of the Support Center website.

Such files can be included into the source texts of Expert Advisors, scripts, custom indicators, and libraries at the compliing<br>include files can be stored in the same directory as a source file - in this case the Finclude directive with double quotes is used.
