To order advertising, go to the [App Store](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/market/product/542) section of the Support Center website.

#### **Conditions**

Each website has only one advertising zone. You can buy the contained impressions partially or fully.

The banner rotation is performed monthly and should be a multiple of 100,000 views.

| Website                    | Cost per 1,000 impressions (CPM) |
|----------------------------|----------------------------------|
| www.mql5.com               | 10 USD                           |
| www.mql4.com               | 10 USD                           |
| www.dolar-colombia.com     | 5 USD                            |
| www.currencyconverterx.com | 5 USD                            |

Advertising is sold on a prepaid basis and thus you need to pay in advance to arrange placement for the coming month. If you have a long-term contract for six or more months, you can pay in monthly installments.

We guarantee that you will get no less than the purchased impression volume. As a bonus, you will receive a fully functional interactive advertising campaign report, compiled by utilizing the [Finteza analytical system.](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/analytics)

![](_page_143_Figure_11.jpeg)

You can estimate the reach, impression frequency and CTR, as well as the parameters of the audience, who actually viewed your banners. Find out who these people are, where they come from, what language they speak, what devices they use, how good the traffic really is (real people, suspicious visits, bots) and much more.

The number of simultaneously running banners is not limited. We recommend adding as many banners as possible. You will be able to disable low-CTR ads, in an effort to increase the display time of ads which accompany the highest conversion rates.

## **Banner formats**

Common banner formats are supported: non-animated GIF, JPG, PNG and HTML5. The responsive format can be used to create ads of different sizes. Finteza automatically generates ready-to-use banners based on uploaded text and images.

![](_page_144_Picture_5.jpeg)

The cost of advertising includes not only desktop banners, but also mobile ones, and there is no difference between them. If you use responsive banners, you do not need to create ready-made banners, while they are created automatically. If you want extra personalization and branding, please use the following formats:

| Banner type    | Banner size    | Valid format                                     |
|----------------|----------------|--------------------------------------------------|
| Desktop/tablet | 800x80         | Responsive, non-animated GIF, JPG, PNG and HTML5 |
| Mobile         | Variable x 80* | Responsive or HTML5                              |

\* Since the width of mobile devices may vary, the mobile banner format accommodates a variable width. The height remains equal to 80 pixels.

# <span id="page-146-0"></span>**White Label partners**

Large and medium-sized brokerage companies often co-operate with other financial organizations. If an emerging company cannot afford to buy a trading platform and arrange all necessary infrastructure, it can conclude a partnership agreement with a larger broker and operate under that broker's White Label. For a certain fee (for example, commission charged for trades), the larger broker provides the technological basis, while the emerging company only has to attract and render services to its clients.

Order an additional White Label to enable your partner to work with its own brand name. In fact, this is a client terminal in which your company's name, logos and contacts are replaced with the relevant details of your affiliate. After receiving the White Label terminal, configure the trading platform, including client groups, financial instruments and manager accounts, for your WL-partner.

## **Order a White Label**

To order a client terminal White Label, go to the Support Center [App Store](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/market) and select Buy next to the MetaTrader 5 Terminal product. In the window that appears, specify the name of the company, for which the White Label is ordered:

# Select Label:

*No additional text provided in the image.*

# MetaQuotes

# Text Content

```
2 000 USD

Total: 2 000 USD

I expressly acknowledge that I have read, and do hereby accept the terms and conditions contained in [this agreement](#). I also expressly acknowledge and agree that by accepting it, I do so on behalf, and with the express authorization, of the Licensee, who shall be legally bound by this Agreement in respect of the use of the Product.

# Cancel

# Confirm your order

The product will be added to your monthly statement

The company name can be entered only in Latin or Cyrillic writing and may contain the following special characters: space ' () + , - . / : = ?.

Be careful when specifying the company name. After your White Label is delivered, you will be able to change the company name only by requesting a new White Label and canceling the current one. In that case your company will have to pay for ordering the new White Label.

After confirming the order, a new request with the order ID in the header and an online White Label creation form is automatically generated in the [Service Desk](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/servicedesk) section of the technical support website. The request will allow you to track your order and provide additional data if necessary.

The online form consists of a few sections: "About window", "Installer window", "Other settings", "Custom files", "Documents", and "Servers". All the form fields are provided with hints. Hover over the field name to view them.

When filling out the form, please pay attention to the following:

- ·All form fields are obligatory.
- · All terminal information (branding, images, server name) must correspond to the company name or its registered brand/business name. The images shall not include any additional marketing slogans unless legally and validly registered/licensed by the company.
- · If you do not have ready logos, you may use the standard MetaQuotes' logos. Logos and other information can be changed later. If you want to update the White Label, navigate to the [App Store / White Labels /](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/market/whitelabel/mt5) [MetaTrader 5](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/market/whitelabel/mt5) section, find the relevant label and click the edit icon next to the company name to open the White Label editing form.

#### **Icons**

This is where you set your terminal logo (icon).

Use an ICO file containing a set of frames sized 16x16, 32x32, 48x48 and 64x64 pixels, with a color depth of 8 bits and 32 bits each.

The icon will appear in the main window of the client terminal, on the desktop and in the Start menu. The icon file - terminal.ico - is located in Client Terminal installation folder.

Icons can be created using various free programs, such as [IcoFX](https://www.icofx.ro/) and others.

See also: [Articles / How to create an icon for the client terminal?](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/articles/1460)

#### **Custom files**

Here, you can upload files which will be automatically added to appropriate folders of the client terminal build.

Custom files should be taken from the similarly-named client terminal folders. Open the terminal, configure it the way you want, close and copy the files. After that, they can be uploaded via the interface for adding custom files. Do not add the files having unfamiliar assignment. Do not fill in this tab if you want to leave the look of the client terminal and all its settings unchanged.

For<br>example, terminal.ini file contains data on location and size of the client<br>terminal windows (however, data on location and size of chart windows<br>are stored in the profile, see the details below). Note that coordinates<br>have absolute values and location of client terminal windows may be<br>different on different monitors. We do not recommend adding this file to<br>the build. The file named trade.ini contains trading settings of the<br>terminal (Tools - Options). The common.ini file contains the main<br>settings of the terminal (Tools - Options). |

| Folder                       | File types                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
|------------------------------|--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|

| MQL5\profiles\templates      | You can add here your own chart templates (*.tpl files), and templates for client terminal reports (*.htm files). Also, you can modify standard report files ReportTrade.htm, ReportHistory.htm or ReportTester.htm and upload them to this folder.          |

#### **Servers**

On this tab, select servers that will appear in the list of available servers when opening an account from the terminal. All your servers will be available in the list, including additional servers.

#### **Documents**

Upload the following documents confirming the registration data of the White Label company:

- ·Certificate of Incorporation
- ·Register of Directors documents
- ·Notarized copy of your IB's Director's passport
- ·Certificate of Good Standing or Certificate of Incumbency
- ·Domain ownership; the website must be registered under the White Label company
- ·Completed and signed KYC form which is provided in the order

If you are ordering an additional White Label for the principal company or for another existing White Label whose documents were provided earlier, the re-uploading of scanned copies of the documents is not required.

#### **Sending data**

Please carefully check all the data before sending the final version. After sending the data, you will be able to change it only after the client terminal is assembled. You may click "Save as a draft" to save changes and return to the form later.

Once you have checked the White Label form data, click "Send final version" at the bottom right corner of the form.

After order confirmation (status of the automatically generated request with the order ID changes to *Completed*), White Label of the client terminal is generated based on the information provided in the online form. The installer download link will become available on the [App Store / White Labels / MetaTrader 5](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/market/whitelabel/mt5) page of the Support Center section.

We strongly recommend that all brokers use the download links that are available in the [White Label](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/market/whitelabel/mt5) section. If your corporate policy does not allow to download files from other sites, you can copy the required files to your website and update them when necessary. Do not forget to update your applications.

See also: [Articles / Why you need to update all links to MetaTrader 4/5 installers](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/articles/427)

In case of a mismatch, the name of the main company will be displayed in the White Label platform instead of the White Label company name.

Specify the White Label company's technical support email and enable the "Send copies to support email" option on the group's Reports tab to enable the sending of the report copies to the White Label company.

The web page specified in the "Support site" field will be displayed on the Company tab of your White Label client terminals. For further configuration details please see the [Company page](#page-77-0) section.

Custom welcome email templates can be used for the White Label company. Open the email template directory on the trade server, for example, <main trade server>\templates\greeting\. Create a new subdirectory and save welcome email templates for the White Label there. Specify the subdirectory name in the "Templates folder" parameter. For further details please read the [Welcome emails](#page-72-0) section.

Commission settings can be defined in advance. Optionally, the White Label company's employees can configure commissions via the Manager terminal. This will require enabling the "Change groups" permissions for the relevant manager account via the MetaTrader 5 Administrator. See below for more details on how to create White Label manager accounts.

#### **Configure opening of demo accounts via White Label terminals in the appropriate groups**

Demo account opening settings are configured in the "Clients & Accounts \ Allocations" section in MetaTrader 5 Administrator.

Two options are available: opening via the client terminal and redirecting to a website. The first option is preferable as the process is fast and convenient.

On the website itself, you have to re-direct customers to different web pages depending on what terminal has been used to open an account. The terminal label can be defined by the additional "label" parameter automatically added to the link on the client terminal side. Below is a sample link that can be generated when opening a demo account from the ABC Company terminal:

https://www.forexbroker.com/open\_account?type=demo&acp=1251&label=ABC Company& server=Forex Broker&interface=English&cid=3c8e1d9cd303dbd0d5686b729689d556

Below is a list of all parameters passed in the link:

- ·first\_name — the first name specified in the registration form on the client terminal side.
- ·second\_name — the last name specified in the registration form on the client terminal side.
- ·email — the email specified in the registration form on the client terminal side.
- ·phone — the phone number specified in the registration form on the client terminal side.
- ·age — the number of days since the installation of the client terminal.
- ·type — the type of the requested account: demo or real.
- ·acp — the code page used by the trader.
- ·label — the name of the company which owns the terminal White Label.
- ·server — the name of the server a trader has selected when opening an account.
- ·interface — the language of the client terminal interface.
- ·cid — the unique identifier of the trader's computer.

When forming a request, the presence of character "?" in the "Account allocation URL" parameter is checked. In other words, it is checked whether the specified address contains its own request parameters:

- · If there is no "?" in the address, it is formed with the standard set of parameters described above. For example, https://www.mycompany.com.
- · If "?" is present in the address, standard parameters are added to the specified custom ones. For example, if address https://www.mycompany.com?utm\_campaign=terminal is specified, the resulting string will be https://www.mycompany.com?utm\_campaign=terminal&type=demo&acp=.... .

Also please pay attention to the following features:

- ·The link address must begin with "https://" or "http://".
- · The redirect function only works for demo accounts. Preliminary accounts can only be opened from client terminals.
- · The "Demo account allocation URL" option is only used in the desktop client terminal versions. Redirection to an external site is not supported in mobile terminals.

#### **Create a manager account for your White Label partner with the appropriate group permissions**

Create a [new account](#page-184-0) in the manager group. If you work with multiple agents or Introducing Brokers, it would be reasonable to create separate manager subgroups for each of them, for example, managers\ABC\_Company.

In the manager group settings, you can specify a custom set of symbols to be processed by the company managers. Only the specified symbols will be available to the dealer in the Market Watch of the Manager terminal.

Next, create a manager account based on this account. Set only the groups previously created for this label to be available for the account.

![](_page_157_Picture_5.jpeg)

Be sure to disable:

- ·Ability to connect using MetaTrader 5 Administrator
- ·The entire "Configuration setup" block of permissions
- ·The entire "Administration" block

You are free to enable other permissions if required by the agreement with your White Label affiliate.

Please be extremely careful when enabling permissions for your White Label's manager account in order to prevent unauthorized access to the critical data and platform settings. Make sure that the White Label company has access only to the customers within its own groups.

See also: [Documentation / Platform / Platform settings / Managers](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/docs/mt5/platform/administration/admin_managers)

# <span id="page-159-0"></span>**MQL5.community services**

[MQL5.community](https://www.mql5.com/) connects millions of traders around the world. Being an integral part of the MetaTrader 5 trading platform, the community provides access to a variety of unique services. Here traders can purchase trading robots and indicators, copy trades of professional traders in the automated mode, rent a virtual server and much more. Users can access all these unique services with a [single MQL5 account.](https://www.mql5.com/en/auth_register)

MQL5.community services are available straight from trading terminals. Traders can specify the MQL5 account login and password in platform settings, and purchase robots straight from the terminals. Also, the account enables users to communicate with fellow traders and receive notifications on their smartphone. An MQL5 account is a pass to all MetaTrader 5 services.

The availability of such services is very important when attracting new customers, as they can assist users in getting started. Inform your traders about the MQL5.community and additional trading services.

# **MQL5 Market: The largest store of applications for MetaTrader**

[MQL5 Market](https://www.mql5.com/en/market) is the largest store of trading applications for the MetaTrader 4 and MetaTrader 5 platforms. The market features over 20,000 trading robots, indicators and other trading programs developed by independent programmers. Dozens of new products and updates of existing applications appear in the Market every day.

![](_page_159_Picture_7.jpeg)

The MQL5 Market is available straight from MetaTrader terminals. After paying for a purchased product using popular payment systems or a credit card, the trader can launch the program on the chart right away. The

purchasing procedure is absolutely safe: payments are performed through an encrypted connection, while the delivery of the application to the terminal is guaranteed by the service.

All trading robots and indicators provide trial versions, which can be downloaded and tested for free in the strategy tester.

All programs downloaded from the Market are securely encrypted. This is to protect them from unauthorized use. Encryption is performed so that the product can be run only on the computer from which it is downloaded. The process of product binding to a computer is called activation.

Every product in the Market has at least five activations: one is used during purchase, and the other four activations are additional. A seller may choose to increase the number of available activations. The number of available activations is determined at the time of purchase; the relevant number is displayed on the product page. Even if the seller changes the number of activations in the future, user purchases will not be affected.

If the Market product can no longer be run on a chart and the terminal journal shows the "invalid license" error, it means that the trader's computer configuration has changed, and thus the product needs to be re-activated. To do this, the user should delete the old file, then open the "Navigator \ Market \ My Purchases" section and install it again.

See also: [Documentation / Client terminal / Market App Store](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/docs/mt5/client/market)

## <span id="page-160-0"></span>**Trading signals and copy trading**

[Signals](https://www.mql5.com/en/signals) is a convenient service for copying trading operations performed by professional traders to a MetaTrader account in the automated mode. The service is available straight from MetaTrader terminals.

A trader can select a suitable signal from the showcase, pay for the subscription in the terminal, and start receiving real-time trades right away. Users can choose from several thousands of signal providers based on detailed monitoring information, which includes the full account history, statistics and charts.

To avoid increased risk, the system does not allow real accounts to subscribe to signals from demo accounts. Demo accounts can subscribe to signals from real accounts.

The transparent signal operation model serves to avoid conflict of interests. The trader pays a fixed amount for a signal subscription, without any additional fees on profit or number of copied trades.

Becoming a signal provider is easy. The trader should specify the MetaTrader account number, investor password and the broker server name (the main account password is not required, so the account is protected). The service connects to the broker server and transmits to subscribers the information about all deals performed on the provider's account. The service architecture based on a global distributed network of servers provides minimum delays when copying operations.

### **Virtual hosting for 24/7 operation**

[Virtual hosting](https://www.mql5.com/en/vps) enables the round-the-clock, failure-free operation of trading robots and signal subscriptions, even when the computer is turned off. The service is easy-to-use and is available directly from the MetaTrader 4/5 terminal. The trader chooses a subscription plan that costs no more than 15 USD, and the service finds a server located close to the broker, to minimize network delays. Trading robots and signal subscriptions can be configured in the terminal and then migrated to a virtual server with a single click.

Service features:

- · Smart resource allocation algorithms ensure that each virtual platform receives enough resources: up to 3 GB of RAM, up to 16 GB of hard disk space and several CPUs allocated on demand.
- ·No additional activations are used for Market applications on the VPS.
- ·Only one account can run on a VPS, however the VPS can be transferred to another account.
- · VPS subscription can be canceled within 24 hours with a full refund. If the subscription is canceled later, the remaining time is credited to the trader's MQL5 account in the form of free minutes. A new VPS can be rented for free using these minutes.

Read more about the service in the [Sponsored VPS](#page-168-0) section.

## **Freelance for ordering trading apps**

A lot of MQL5.community members have vast programming experience and are willing to develop custom orders. [MQL5 Freelance](https://www.mql5.com/en/job) is a secure service for ordering custom applications from freelancers. Any type of software, from custom Expert Advisors and indicators for MetaTrader 4/5 to a whole website or a Windows app in C++, can be ordered from here.

The price of order development starts from USD 30. Choose from more than 700 developers of different skill level, from beginners to professionals, who can program in MQL4/MQL5, as well as in other popular programming languages, such as C++, C#, PHP, Java, Python, and others.

The service proved a transparent and safe system for both parties. Orders are performed in steps, starting with the selection of the developer and discussion of preliminary specifications and finishing with the acceptance of the final work. The agreed order cost is frozen on the customer's account for the order development time. This procedure is aimed at protecting both parties: it guarantees payment for a properly developed order while ensuring the development of the ordered application.

In the Freelance service, you can order branded applications for MetaTrader 4/5 and provide them free of charge to your traders by adding them to terminals via a [White Label](#page-146-0)form. You can also find programmers for your specific needs, as well as translators having vast experience in IT, finance and trading related translations.

![](_page_164_Picture_6.jpeg)

## **Economic Calendar**

The [Economic Calendar](https://www.mql5.com/en/economic-calendar) provides up-to-date real-time information on more than 800 macroeconomic events of the largest world economies, including the United States, the European Union, the United Kingdom, Japan and Canada. Information about economic indicator releases can assist users in forecasting future market developments and in formulating informed trading decisions.

The calendar is MetaQuotes' proprietary solution. We monitor the publication of macroeconomic indicators from primary sources in real time and invite you to broadcast this valuable data on your website. This is a legally clean service, with no licensing risks or intermediaries. Install our economic calendar and equip your traders with essential fundamental information on the spot, so that there is no need for your clients to visit competitor or third-party resources.

The economic calendar will assist your traders in preparing forecasts and in making informed trading decisions. Any financial assets, including currencies, stocks, indices, metals and oil, respond to news releases and statistical publications.

#### **Fast and easy installation**

To install the calendar, use the [Add to website](https://www.mql5.com/en/economic-calendar/widgets) button on the Calendar page of the MQL5.com website. Specify the desired size of the calendar and the display period for upcoming events, which can be the current day or week. All changes are instantly reflected in the Preview section, which visualizes how the calendar will appear on your website page. The calendar widget is currently available in ten languages:

- ·English, Russian, Chinese, Spanish, Portuguese
- ·German, Japanese, Arabic, Turkish, Korean

Here is an example of an embed code for the economic calendar in English, featuring current week events:

```
<script type="text/javascript"
src="https://www.tradays.com/c/js/widgets/calendar/widget.js?8"></script>
<div id="economicCalendarWidget"></div>
<script type="text/javascript">
  new economicCalendar({ width: "100%", height: "100%", mode: 2, lang: "en" });
</script>
```

Copy the auto-generated code and paste it into your webpage. This completes the installation of the calendar.

![](_page_166_Picture_6.jpeg)

#### **Advantages of our economic calendar**

· Free: we have developed this calendar for the MetaTrader 5 trading platform and allow our customers to install it for free.

- ·No licensing risks: we collect all the relevant data from primary sources using specialized software.
- · Support for ten languages: all events and descriptions are translated into the most common global languages, with more translations to be added later.
- · Full representation: every event is provided with a detailed description and notes related to the release influence on individual currencies, as well as the source link, release date and charts.
- ·No ads: you will not have to pay for the service by showing third-party ads.
- · Automatic time zone selection: event tracking is maintained via local time adaptation. Manual adaptation of time zones is also possible.
- ·Automatic update: the calendar is automatically updated as soon as a new event appears in a source.
- · Continuous development: when a country is added, all economic indices which have a significant impact on the national currency are included in the calendar.
- ·Simple and quick installation: the calendar is installed in just a few clicks.

#### **MQL5.community business account**

Extended options are available for brokerage accounts at MQL5.community: [managing signals and subscriptions](#page-160-0) available to your traders, creating and topping up MQL5 accounts via the API.

By creating an MQL5 account with an initial top-up, you can help your traders get started with the website and related services. The MQL5 account creation procedure can be implemented in the trader's room or on a broker's website. It means that your clients having a MetaTrader account and a profile on your site, will be able to subscribe to trading signals, purchase applications from the Market, and access other trading services.

The above options are available from a brokerage account. [Create](https://www.mql5.com/en/auth_register) an account on mql5.com and [contact us via](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/servicedesk) [Service Desk](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/servicedesk) in order to obtain extended permissions.

For API usage details, please see [Documentation](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/docs/community/mql5_api).

# <span id="page-168-0"></span>**Sponsored VPS**

The [Virtual Hosting](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/docs/mt5/client/virtual_hosting) service is extremely important for traders. It enables 24/7 operation of trading robots and [copied signals](https://www.mql5.com/en/signals), without the need to keep the computer turned on permanently. Furthermore, users do not have to worry about the hardware and connections.

The service is available directly from the client terminals, so traders can start using it in just a few clicks. The service ensures minimal network delays between the terminal and the broker's server, as the system selects the nearest hosting server automatically.

Sponsored Hosting provides a great tool for attracting new traders. Start providing a free useful service to your clients and create a competitive edge to attract more traders. By providing stable and continuous trading 24/7 for existing clients, you will increase trading volumes.

#### See also:

- ·[Articles / Gain a competitive edge with MetaQuotes VPS](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/news/3439)
- ·[Articles / 4 reasons to tell your traders about VPS for MetaTrader](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/news/3394)
- ·[Articles / Enable VPS for your traders — it pays off](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/articles/1018)

#### **Preparation**

A [corporate MQL5.community account](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/docs/community/mql5_api) is needed to pay for the Sponsored VPS. Your traders' hosting rentals will be paid from the balance of this account.

Such an account is automatically created for you and is added to the Sponsored VPS section in the Platform. This is absolutely safe. The data is securely encrypted, while the created account is not publicly available on MQL5.com to other users and to search engines.

If necessary, you can create another corporate account to manage the payments. To do this, register a regular MQL5.com account and contact our [Service Desk](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/servicedesk/new) to expand permissions. Next, add your account to the Sponsored Hosting section.

### **Paying for VPS**

Payments for VPS are made from the balance of your corporate MQL5.com account. The current balance is displayed at the bottom of the section. Please make sure you always have enough money on this account, otherwise your traders will not be able to rent new sponsored VPSs.

![](_page_169_Picture_1.jpeg)

You can top up your balance in two ways:

- · Via the [payment systems](https://www.mql5.com/en/articles/302) available at MQL5.com. There is no need to go to the site, while the money can be added by using the "Top up" button at the bottom of the page.
- · By purchasing the ["Sponsored Virtual Hosting for MetaTrader 5"](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/market/product/617) product from the Support Center App Store. This option allows you to fund the account via a company invoice. You can request a top-up for the required amount and pay the invoice, after which the relevant funds will be credited to your MQL5 account. An additional Sponsored VPS service agreement can be concluded if necessary. The relevant details should be provided in the ticket created upon product order.

The entire history of top-ups and hosting payments will be available in your MQL5 profile, under the "Payments" section.

# **Platform setup**

To manage sponsorship, use the Integrations \ VPS section in MetaTrader 5 Administrator.

![](_page_170_Picture_3.jpeg)

Enter your MQL5 account to be able to monitor the remaining balance, as well as to see the number of VPSs allocated to your traders.

Next, enable the "Activate sponsored hosting" option and specify the list of groups for which the service will be available. Additional parameters can be specified for each group:

- · Minimum balance — the minimum client balance value with which a sponsored VPS is available to the trader. Use to reward your major customers.
- · Cancel after, days — if there is no trading activity on the account for the specified period, the free VPS will be canceled.

Access can be additionally controlled at the trading account level, using the "Sponsored MetaTrader Virtual Hosting" option:

We have added extra protection to avoid service misuse:

- · Only one sponsored hosting server can be rented for each account at a time, while the allocation of multiple servers is prohibited
- ·No other account can be launched on the sponsored hosting server

For further details on the Virtual Hosting service please refer to the [client terminal documentation.](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/docs/mt5/client/virtual_hosting)

# <span id="page-174-0"></span>**Common errors**

- · Do not distribute client terminal installers through your website. We strongly recommend using installer download links from our CDN network. The network consists of a dozen of access points around the world, to ensure reliability and maximum download speed. Installers on such links are always up to date so you do not have to update them manually. Furthermore, you will receive extra analytical capabilities through UTM parameters. For further details please read the article [Why you need to update all links to MetaTrader 4/5](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/articles/427) [installers.](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/articles/427)
- · Do not forget to set up groups for your White Label on the trade server side, to avoid the "invalid server name" error in your White Label's mobile terminals when opening accounts.
- · When you reinstall the platform, your server becomes available in the web terminal within 24 hours. Likewise for the demo account request window in the web terminal: after you enable the allocation of demo accounts on the server, the server will become available in the list within 24 hours.
- · UTM parameters from the terminal installation links are registered in accounts only if you have an active Finteza license.

# <span id="page-175-0"></span>**Working with trading accounts**

Through the manager terminal, you can perform all operations related to customer service: open and configure accounts, deposit funds and execute trading operations on them. The Manager terminal provides access to full client information, including personal data, all open positions and orders, financial status and the history of all trading operations.

Also, the terminal shows accounts currently connected to the trading servers.

Various commands are available in the context menu of the Clients, Accounts and Online Users sections:

- ·The Column command enables the management of the displayed information.
- ·Commands Notification and E-Mail allow sending [information to clients](#page-63-0).
- ·Details on client operations can be requested from the trading server log using the Journal command.

- · The Balances submenu enables the quick [balance check based on the operations history](#page-214-0), after which you can make the relevant balance corrections.
- · The "Select by" submenu enables convenient selection of clients and accounts in the list. Copy logins to the clipboard and click "Select By \ Logins from Clipboard" in the context menu. Accounts with the relevant login numbers will be instantly found and selected in the list. Similarly, you can use a list of accounts from a text file.

Using the context menu, it is possible to [export](#page-111-0) and [import accounts](#page-186-0). If Autoscroll is enabled, the list of accounts will be scrolled to the last account every time a new trading account is added to the list. The Autoscroll option only works if the account list is sorted by login.

## <span id="page-177-0"></span>**Filtering accounts and clients**

Use filters for convenient operations with clients and accounts. By default, the list shows all [clients](#page-6-0) and accounts available to the manager. You may use filters to view information that meets certain criteria. For example, you can view clients from a selected country, clients from specific marketing campaigns, or accounts with a certain language.

To apply a previously created filter, select it from the Filter menu in the list of accounts or clients. To return to the original list of accounts, click "Not selected".

Set the name of the filter and then configure parameters for filtering accounts:

- ·**Hide disabled accounts** — display only enabled accounts in the list.
- ·**Trading** — show accounts with the enabled or disabled trading option.
- · **Country, City, Language** — show accounts from the selected country, city or with the selected language. For example, such filters can be useful when sending bulk notification to clients, such as emails or push notifications.
- · **Agent account** — show only accounts of the specified agent in the list. Use this filter to analyze the work of agents.

- · **Group** — show only accounts from the specified group. This filter can be used to separate work with real and demo accounts.
- · **MetaQuotes ID** — show accounts with this [MetaQuotes ID](#page-191-1) or all accounts which have any MetaQuotes ID. In the latter case, leave the field empty and invert the filter using the button.
- · **Company** — show accounts which have the selected company specified in their parameters. This is a useful filter for working with corporate clients.
- · **Lead campaign, Lead source** — show accounts with this specific lead campaign and lead source. These filters will be helpful in analyzing the effectiveness of your marketing campaigns.
- · **Comments** — show accounts with the specified comment. The filter allows viewing accounts based on certain internal criteria.
- · **Balance** — show the accounts that have a balance within the specified range. When combined with the registration date filter, this option enables the detection of inactive clients.
- · **Registration** — show the accounts with the registration date within the specified range. This filter can be helpful in working with new clients.
- · **Last access** — show the accounts with the last login date within the specified range. This filter can be helpful in working with inactive/abandoned accounts.

The following filtering options are available for [online accounts:](#page-203-0)

- · **Country, City** — show accounts from the selected country or city. For example, such filters can be useful when sending bulk notification to clients, such as emails or push notifications.
- · **Group** — show only accounts from the specified group. This filter can be used to separate work with real and demo accounts.
- · **Client** — show accounts with the selected connection type: desktop, mobile and manager terminals, API, etc. Use the filter to analyze which features your clients use.
- · **Version** — show accounts which use the specified client application build. The filter can be used to identify clients using old terminal versions.
- · **Balance** — show the accounts that have a balance within the specified range. When combined with the registration date filter, this option enables the detection of inactive clients.
- · **Equity** — show accounts having the equity within the specified range. When combined with the balance filter, this option enables detection of clients with large floating profit or loss.

The following filtering options are available in the list of [clients](#page-6-0):

- · **Country, City, Language** — show clients from the selected country, city or with the selected language. These filters can be useful for audience analysis.
- · **Company** — show clients, in which this company is specified. Such a filter is useful for working with corporate clients.

- · **Introducer** — show only the clients that were introduced by the specified user (the account number is specified). Use this filter to analyze the work of agents.
- · **Assigned manager** — show clients managed by the specified assigned manager. The filter is useful for managers working with clients, as well as for controlling the performance of such managers.
- · **Lead campaign, Lead source** — show clients with this specific lead campaign and lead source. These filters will be helpful in analyzing the effectiveness of your marketing campaigns.
- ·**Type** — display only private or only corporate clients in the list.
- · **Status** — display clients with a selected registration status in the list. For example, the filter can show clients with abandoned registration, inactive clients, etc.

Filters can be immediately enabled from the editing window by clicking Apply.

Filters enable the selection of records not only based on the matching field, but also by the "Except" and "Not empty field" parameters. Specify a value in the filter field and then click icon "+", which will change to "-". The filter will select accounts/clients, the parameters of which do not match the specified value. For example, you can use this option to obtain a list of accounts which do not belong to the specified group, as well as a list of clients without an assigned manager. In the latter case, switch the filter mode and leave the field blank.

# <span id="page-181-0"></span>**Trading account types**

The trading platform supports several account types. For convenience, individual icons are used for different types:

- · — demo accounts to trade in a training mode without investing real money, as well as contest accounts which can be used in various trading contests.
- ·— preliminary accounts which enable quick requesting of real accounts directly from client terminals.
- ·— live accounts for real trading.
- ·— an account for working with traders and for managing the platform.

The account type is determined by the group in which it is located. The system determines the group type by its name.

#### **Demo groups**

Demo groups contain demo accounts. They enable users to work in a training mode without real money, allowing them to test trading strategies. These accounts have the same functionality as the real ones.

The difference is that demo accounts can be opened without any investment and, therefore, one cannot expect to profit from them. Demo accounts can be opened directly from client terminals, while real accounts can only be opened through Manager and Administrator terminals.

A created group is considered a demo group if its name (including the path) contains the characters "demo" (case sensitive). For example, demo\forex, demo-USD and real\demoforex-USD are demo groups, while Demoforex and fx-USD are not considered as demo.

For further details please see the [Demo account allocation](#page-7-0)section.

#### **Manager groups**

Accounts for broker employees are created in manager groups. Only such accounts can connect to trading servers via Administrator and Manager terminals or via the API.

A group is considered as manager if its name (including path) contains "manager" (case sensitive). For example, manager\_CFD or manager\_real.

#### **Contest groups**

This type is provided specifically for holding contests among traders. A group is considered as contest if its name (including path) contains "contest" (case sensitive). For example, contest\_forex or contest\USD.

Contest accounts cannot be opened from terminals, as they can only be created by the broker.

Contest accounts are marked with a blue icon in client terminals. When a trader is connected to such an account, the "Contest account" title is displayed in the terminal window header. In some reports, contest accounts can be displayed in separate groups. Contest accounts operate similarly to demo accounts.

#### **Preliminary groups**

One preliminary group is created automatically on the server. When a trader requests a real account from the client terminal, a preliminary account is created in this group.

Managers can monitor the creation of such accounts and contact clients using the contact details provided during registration. After completing formal procedures, the manager can simply move a preliminary account in one of the real groups, after which the client will be able to trade.

You can create additional preliminary groups so that customers can immediately request specific account types.

Features of preliminary groups:

- ·Trading is prohibited for all symbols in the preliminary group.
- ·All accounts in such groups are created with a zero balance.
- · When opening an account in this group, the client receives a standard email from the [main server directory] \templates\greeting\preliminary\ folder.

For further details please see the [Preliminary account allocation](#page-17-0)section.

#### **Coverage groups**

The MetaTrader 5 trading platform enables the covering of client positions on other trading servers to protect a company from risks. When a client opens a position, the broker can open a position on another broker's server. Such trading operations are performed through coverage groups.

Coverage groups are included in special reports, while their positions are displayed in a separate tab in the Manager terminal enabling the broker's risk manager to control situation.

The names of coverage groups (including path) contain "coverage" (case sensitive). For example, coverage\forex.

See also: [Documentation / Manager / Dealing and risk management / Summary positions and coverage](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/docs/mt5/manager/summary_positions)

## **Real groups**

These groups contain accounts for live financial trading using real money. If a group does not fall into any of the above categories, the system determines the group as real.

- · If groups in the system have a hierarchy, their names should contain the relevant path. This path is an integral part of the group name, which is taken into account when determining its type. For example, all the subgroups that are created in the group "manager", will be considered manager groups.
- ·Do not mix signs of different group types within one group name. For example, managers\demo.

For convenience, the account settings in the account creation window are divided into two blocks.

#### **Information**

This block provides information about a client:

- · **Login** — account number. If you specify "next" instead of the number, the next available number will be assigned to the account.
- · **Group** — the group in which the account will be created. The manager can only select the group to which they have access.

- · **Client** — the [client](#page-6-0) to whom the account should be linked. Specify the identifier, the name, or contact information in the "Existing client" field and then click Request. Next, select the required client from the list of found records.
- ·**First name** — the first name of the account holder.
- ·**Last name** — the last name of the account holder.
- ·**Middle name** — the middle name of the account holder.
- ·**Company** — the name of the client's company.
- ·**E-Mail** — e-mail address.
- ·**Phone** — phone number.
- ·**Country** — country of residence.
- ·**State** — state (region) of residence.
- ·**City/Town** — city/town of residence.
- ·**Zip code** — zip or postal code.
- ·**Address** — client's address.

After creating an account, you can specify [additional settings](#page-189-0) for it.

Accounts are always created with a zero balance. To add funds, open the [Balance](#page-205-0) section of the created account.

A manually created account [is not linked to a client record.](#page-35-0) To bind the account manually, open the accounts section of the required customer or create a new client record based on the account.

#### **Passwords**

Account passwords are specified in this block. When you create an account, passwords are generated automatically, but you can specify them manually in the appropriate fields:

- ·**Master** — master password for the account.
- ·**Investor** — investor password (without the ability to perform trading operations).
- · **Phone** — phone password used for identifying the account holder while performing trade operations over the phone.

All passwords, including master and investor, must contain two of the three types of characters (lowercase letters, uppercase letters and digits). The minimum length of a password is determined by group settings on the server. The maximum length is 16 characters.

The following commands are available in the account importing window:

- · **File** — path to the \*.CSV file from which you want to import accounts. Specify the path manually or select the file using the Browse... button located to the right.
- ·**Separator** — data separator in the file (comma, semicolon, tabulation character or space).
- · **Start from line** — the line of the file starting from which data will be imported. For example, if you set 3, the first two lines in the file will be skipped.
- · **Columns...** — open the section where you can [match data](#page-187-0) in the imported file with appropriate fields in the system.
- · **Use selected items only** — if enabled, only rows currently selected in the preview window below will be imported.
- ·**Refresh** — refresh data in the preview window.

To see how the account will look after import, double-click on it in the list or select View in its context menu. Click Apply to import accounts. The import progress will be displayed in the Journal tab the Toolbox window.

#### **Import features**

- · All accounts are imported with the disabled status. Further, they can be enabled manually from the Accounts tab.
- ·Clients' financial data (balance) and security data cannot be imported.
- ·Set passwords for accounts after import.

### <span id="page-187-0"></span>**Data matching**

This function allows the matching of data in the imported file with the data fields of client records on the trading platform side. To start matching, click Columns... in the import window.

In the right part of the window, arrange columns in accordance with how they are located in the imported file:

- ·**Add** — add the column to selected ones.
- · **Remove** — remove the column from the list of selected ones. The Login field is required, it cannot be removed.
- ·**Up** — move the added column up relative to other columns.
- ·**Down** — move the added column down relative to other columns.
- ·**Reset** — return default column settings.

You can also move columns by double clicking on them.

The sequence of selected columns is very important, because this is how they will be matched with the fields in the system. To skip one or more columns in the source file, use the special "Skip column" option. For example, if this item is set third from top in the sequence, the third column in the imported file will be skipped.

### **Account overview**

The Overview tab displays the general information about the account: personal data of the account holder, last connection date, and current trading state. Last connection time and address data is updated once per hour.

If you have an active Finteza subscription, this section can additionally display user tracking data: Visitor ID and Affiliate. For further details please see the [Finteza end-to-end analytics](#page-97-0) section.

The lower part displays the account trading status:

- ·Open positions for financial instruments
- ·Financial condition of the account
- ·Assets in the form of non-tradable instruments
- ·Active pending orders

The following data is specified here:

- ·**First name** — the first name of the account holder.
- ·**Last name** — the last name of the account holder.
- ·**Middle name**— the middle name of the account holder.
- ·**Company** — name of the account holder's company.
- · **Registered** — account registration date. The date is added automatically during account creation. Please be careful when changing the registration date manually: data should fit the account trading history, and thus there should not be any trading operations before the registration date. Otherwise, such operations can be ignored when generating [reports](#page-81-0).
- ·**Language** — user's language.
- ·**Status** — status of the account holder, RE (resident) or NR (non-resident).

<span id="page-191-0"></span>· **Lead Source, Lead Campaign** — the website from which the client came (lead source) and the name of the marketing campaign, as a result of which the client came.

These fields enable the analysis of marketing campaigns and tracking of leads. To receive the data, add the special parameters to the client or mobile platform download links:

https://download.mql5.com/cdn/web/metaquotes.ltd/mt5/mt5setup.exe? utm\_source=YourWebsite&utm\_campaign=YourCampaign https://download.mql5.com/cdn/mobile/mt5/ios?server=ABC-Demo,ABC-Real&utm\_source=YourWebsite&utm\_campaign=YourCampaign https://download.mql5.com/cdn/mobile/mt5/android?server=ABC-Demo,ABC-Real&utm\_source=YourWebsite&utm\_campaign=YourCampaign

Here YourCampaign is the company name, and YourWebsite is the address of the website hosting the link. In the 'server' parameter of the mobile platform links, enter the list of your servers to be shown to traders when they open an account.

When opening a demo account and connecting to any trading account via the terminal downloaded using such a link, utm\_source and utm\_campaign values will be registered in the client record on the server side. If the fields are already filled, the label values will not be overwritten when the account reconnects (even when connecting from a terminal downloaded by a link with other labels).

Lead Source and Lead Campaign data from terminal download links is registered in created accounts only if you have a [Finteza license](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/docs/mt5/platform/administration/integration/integration_finteza).

- ·**ID number** — passport number, tax ID or any other unique identifiers of the account holder.
- <span id="page-191-1"></span>· **MetaQuotes ID** — during installation of MetaTrader 5 mobile terminals for [iPhone](https://download.mql5.com/cdn/mobile/mt5/ios?hl=ru&utm_campaign=download&utm_source=metatrader5.help) or [Android](https://download.mql5.com/cdn/mobile/mt5/android?hl=ru&utm_campaign=download&utm_source=metatrader5.help) a unique MetaQuotes ID is provided to every user. This ID is used similarly to a phone number, enabling the receiving of [push notifications](#page-69-0).

MetaQuotes ID is registered in the account on the broker's side when the client specifies it in the desktop terminal settings or connects to the account via a mobile terminal.

- ·**E-mail** — email address.
- ·**Phone** — phone number.
- ·**Country** — country of residence. Affects the display of additional [disclaimers or risk warnings](#page-195-0).
- ·**State** — state (region) of residence.
- ·**City/Town** — city/town of residence.
- ·**Zip code** — zip or postal code.
- ·**Address** — client's address.
- ·**Comment** — a text comment to the account.

When opening a demo account from the client terminal, the client's country and city/town are filled based on GeoIP data. If GeoIP detection is not possible, the language of the client's operating system is used.

It contains the following parameters:

- ·**Group** — the group to which the account belongs.
- · **Color** — the color that will be used for the trading request from the client displayed in the manager terminal. For example, you can mark suspicious and VIP clients with certain colors.
- ·**Leverage** — leverage of the account.
- ·**Bank account** — the client's account in an external bank.
- ·**Agent account** — account for charging agent commissions on trade operations on this account.
- · **Enable this account** — enable/disable the account. If you uncheck this item, the account will become inactive, and its icon will become gray . It will be impossible to connect to the server using this account. Disabling of an account does not disable the sending of daily or monthly reports to this account. This option should be [disabled separately.](#page-194-0)
- · **Enable password change** — if enabled, an account holder will be able to change account passwords in the client terminal.

- · **Enable one-time password** — this option can be used to disable the use of one-time passwords for separate clients in the group. If OTP use is disabled for the group, enabling this option will have no effect.
- · **Change password at next login** — if you enable this option, the account holder will be prompted to change the master password during the next connection. No actions can be performed on the account until password change. The option will be automatically disabled once the password is changed.

#### **Trading accounts**

The trading platform can be integrated with an external trading system such as a stock exchange or an ECN. Integrations are implemented via gateways, which deliver price data from an external system and forward to it trading operations performed by traders in MetaTrader 5. The special client record field, account number in an external trading system, is used for associating a client in the trading platform with a client in the external system.

The "Trading accounts" section allows the management of client trading account numbers in external systems. Each account number is associated with a specific gateway, through which the platform interacts with the external system.

- · Accounts can be moved between groups with different deposit currency only if their balance is zero and they have no open positions.
- ·Accounts cannot be moved between groups which belong to different trade servers.
- · Do not move accounts between groups with different position accounting systems (netting and hedging), especially if they have open positions. They may become incorrect.

#### **Limits**

|                                                     |                                                |               |                |

In this section, you can configure trading account permissions and restrictions:

- · **Show to regular managers** — this rule allows the convenient working with technical accounts. Disable this option for testing accounts to hide them from all managers who do not have special access to technical accounts (which is provided by the platform administrator). Such technical accounts can be confusing for the managers working with clients, in which case hiding them can be useful. The permission affects the visibility in the general list of accounts in the Administrator and Manager terminals, as well as in the list of online accounts in the Manager terminal.
- · **Include in server reports** — using this option, you can exclude the account from [server reports.](#page-81-0) Like the previous permission, it is intended for more convenient work with technical accounts. Permission to edit the previous two options is provided by the platform administrator.
- <span id="page-194-0"></span>· **Enable daily reports** — allow receiving of [daily and monthly reports](#page-106-0). If this option is disabled, HTML reports for the account will not be generated or sent (this option does not affect the generation of daily data for server reports).
- ·**Enable API connections** — allow connection using this account via Web API.
- · **Enable sponsored VPS hosting** — brokers can pay the [virtual hosting](https://www.mql5.com/en/vps) fee for their client. The service is extremely important for traders, and the opportunity to receive a VPS for free can give them a good reason to choose your company over competitors. The availability of a broker-sponsored VPS is controlled at the individual account level. Only if this option is enabled, the appropriate payment plan will be shown to the trader in the client terminal. For more details, please read the [appropriate section.](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/docs/community/vps#sponsored)
- · **Enable trading** — if disabled, trading on the account is impossible: the user will not be able to place new orders or modify existing orders and positions. However, the account can be used for connecting to the server and analyzing price dynamics. Disabling of trading does not affect the triggering of existing pending orders and of Take Profit and Stop Loss levels of existing positions. The Stop Out procedure is not performed for accounts with disabled trading, because this tool is used for the internal control of broker risks.
- ·**Enable algo trading by Expert Advisors** — allow the account to trade using Expert Advisors.
- ·**Enable trailing stops** — allow the account to use trailing stops in the client terminal.
- · **Limit total value of positions** — the maximum value of open positions allowed on the account. Positions are evaluated as follows:

For each symbol, the total value of positions and active pending orders is determined separately for each direction, i.e., separately for buy and sell operations. The difference in the values of these direction is calculated. A similar analysis is performed for all instruments for which the account has open positions and orders.

The obtained values are summed up and the result is compared with the specified limit. Once the limit is reached, the platform will disable the ability to place new orders if their execution can increase the total value of positions.

The value calculation for each position/order depends on the symbol's margin/profit calculation method.

For the symbols with the Forex calculation type, the value is calculated in the base symbol currency and is equal to the product of the contract size and the volume. For example, for EURUSD with the contract size of 100,000, the value of 1 lot is equal to 100,000 EUR.

For the symbols with the CFD, CFD Leverage, CFD Index and Futures calculation type, the value is also calculated in the deposit currency. Since the contract size of such instruments is not expressed in money (it can be expressed, for example, in the amount of assets), the contract size is additionally multiplied by the instrument price to obtain the value in monetary terms. For Futures symbols, the final value is additionally multiplied by the ratio of tick price to tick size. For example, if some Futures symbol has USD as the base currency, the contract size is equal to 100, the cost is 33, and the tick price to tick size ratio is 1/0.1, the value of one lot of the position is equal to 100\*33\*10 = 33,000 USD. For a СFD symbol with the same parameters, one lot size would be 100\*33 = 3,300 USD.

If the symbol base currency differs from the account deposit currency (specified after the field), the calculated value will be additionally converted using the relevant exchange rate.

· **Limit number of active orders** — the maximum number of active (placed) pending orders allowed on the account. Once the limit is reached, the client will no longer be able to place new pending orders If no value is specified (default), a limit defined at the group level will be used. If limits are set both at the account and at the group level, the stricter one of them will be applied.

### <span id="page-195-0"></span>**Country-specific risk warning and disclaimer**

Every country has regulatory authorities, which are state bodies that supervise the activities of financial institutions (including brokers). Each regulatory authority has its own set of requirements for the organization of Forex, security and other types of financial trading.

If necessary, you can display a risk warning each time the trader connects to the account. The display of the relevant information can be enabled through a special parameter in the account group via the MetaTrader 5 Administrator:

Trading operations on the account are not allowed until the trader confirms that they have read the warning and are aware of the risks. To confirm, the trader should check the option "I am aware of the risks and I wish to trade in financial markets".

Investing in such products might not be suitable for all investors, because it carries a risk of losing a part or all deposited amount. |  |

The possibility exists that you could sustain a loss higher than your initial investment and therefore you should not invest money that you cannot afford to lose.     |  |

This warning is displayed once per session of the terminal. The next time it appears after the restart of the terminal. The warning is not displayed for demo accounts, regardless of group settings.

Often brokerage companies offer services to traders from different countries. The platform provides the ability to meet specific regulatory requirements without interfering with the work of traders from the regions to which these requirements do not apply.

Restrictions applied to trader accounts depend on the client's country and other conditions set by that country's regulator. These restrictions and the relevant countries are already provided in the platform. To enable the mechanism, use parameter "Enforce country-specific regulatory restrictions for retail clients".

For example, according to the National Securities Market Commission (Comisión Nacional del Mercado de Valores), brokers are required to specifically warn clients using leverage 1:10 or higher about risks. Accordingly, if Spain is indicated as the client's country, and the leverage 1:10 or higher is used, an additional warning in the trade dialog will be displayed for that client.

The trading dialog and the risk warning will be shown even when using the one-click trading functions, for example, when trying to perform a trading operation from the Depth of Market or from the on-chart quick trading panel.

Currently, only one regional restriction is used in the platform. The list will be expanded.

# <span id="page-198-0"></span>**Connection security**

The standard account authentication method used in the platform is login and password.

The client's login is an unsigned integer in the range between 1 and 18,446,744,073,709,551,615. The password is a string between 8 and 16 characters long. The minimum password length is configured in the properties of the group to which the client belongs.

The system controls the complexity of all used passwords. A password must contain at least two of three types of characters: lowercase letters, uppercase letters and digits.

You can use extended security measures by enabling the following options:

- · Extended authentication — in addition to the standard login and password, the user will need a special certificate file to connect to the account.
- · One-time passwords — in addition to the standard login and password, the user will need to enter an additional password generated through the mobile application.

#### **Security settings**

The authentication method and other connection settings are configured at individual account group levels via MetaTrader 5 Administrator:

| Symbols                 |  |
| <b>Commissions</b>      |  |
| Reports                 |  |

Group is a set of users that have the same permission settings and service conditions.

**Help**

Go to the Common tab and specify the following information:

- · **Authentication** — authentication method to be used for this group:
  - ·**Normal** — basic authentication using login and password.
  - ·**1024-bit RSA SSL certificate** — extended authentication using certificates with 1024-bit encryption.
  - ·**2048-bit RSA SSL certificate** — extended authentication using certificates with 2048-bit encryption.
  - ·**Custom SSL certificate** — extended authentication using custom certificates.
- · **Minimum password length** — minimum password length for the accounts created in this group and for the certificates used for extended authentication. The maximum possible password length is 16 characters.
- · **Enable certificate confirmation** — if you check the box, then confirmation will be requested for all generated client certificates.
- · **Change password at first login** — if enabled, the user will be requested to change the master password on first connection. No actions can be performed on the account until password change. When enabling this option, please make sure that clients in the group are allowed to change password ("Change password at next login" option is enabled). Otherwise, users will not be able to connect to the server because they will not be able to change the password.

See also: [Documentation / Platform / Platform settings / Groups / Group settings](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/docs/mt5/platform/administration/admin_groups/groups_settings)

#### **Password management**

Up to four types of passwords can be used for each account.

- ·**Master password** provides full access to the account, including trading functions.
- ·**Investor password** provides access to an account without the ability to perform trading operations.
- · **Web API password** is required if the account is used for authorization via a Web client written using MetaTrader 5 Web API.
- ·**Phone password** is used to identify the account holder while performing trade operations over phone.

Account passwords are managed via the Security section.

| Master password is used for full access to the trading account                                    |  |
|---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|--|
| minimum 8 characters                                                                              |  |
| Investor password is used for limited access to the trading account in read-only mode             |  |
| minimum 8 characters                                                                              |  |
| API password is used for access to the server using Web API                                       |  |
| minimum 8 characters                                                                              |  |
| Phone password allows to identify the account owner when performing trade operations by phone     |  |
| to view password set focus to field                                                               |  |
| Shared secret key in combination with the current timestamp is used to generate one-time password |  |
| OTP secret key                                                                                    |  |

Master, investor, and Web API passwords must contain four character types: lowercase letters, uppercase letters, numbers and symbols (#, @, !, etc.). For example, 1Ar#pqkj. The minimum password length is determined by group settings, while the lowest possible value is 8 characters. The maximum length is 16 characters.

To change the password, enter a new password and click Change. The password change result is displayed in the manager terminal journal.

To create a random master password, click Generate. Next, select Change to assign the created password to your account. You can set other passwords in the same way. After generating the password, copy it into the required field.

To find out if the specified password is used for the account, enter the password and click Check. The check result will be displayed in a separate window.

To view a phone password, place the mouse cursor to Password field. To change it, specify a new password and click Update.

#### **OTP Secret Key**

The key is a link between the account and the one-time password generator (mobile terminal) to which it is bound. The key is represented by a sequence of 16 characters generated based on the data about the device the MetaTrader 5 mobile platform is installed on.

Each account can only be bound to one password generator. To rebind the account to a new generator, you will need to enter a one-time code from the previous generator.

If the user no longer has access to the bound password generator (for example, the mobile device is lost), the current OTP secret key can be deleted. In this case, account authorization via one-time passwords will be disabled and the user will be able to bind the account to a new generator.

If the user has forgotten the PIN for the password generator but they use the same mobile device, they can simply reinstall the mobile platform and rebind the account to the generator. No one-time password is required when rebinding the account to a generator on the same device.

See also: [Documentation / Manager / For advanced users / One Time Passwords](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/docs/mt5/manager/beginning_advanced/otp)

### **Certificates**

Use the Certificate section to manage the certificate used for extended account authentication.

The tab shows information about the generated or imported certificate if a certificate is available for the account. This tab includes the account number for which the certificate was generated, the name of the account, and the certification center that issued the certificate. Click on the certificate to open further details in a separate window.

If the standard authentication method is used for the group, the tab is not displayed.

Commands for working with the certificate:

· **Confirm** — you can enable additional certificate confirmation for a client group for increased operation security. The user will not be able to log in using a certificate until it is confirmed. Once the client completes the required steps, the manager can confirm the certificate using this command.

- · **Reset** — if a certificate has already been generated or imported for the account, you can reset this account using this command. In this case, authorization using this certificate will become impossible. If certificates are generated on the server, a new certificate will be assigned to the account on the next connection.
- · **Import** — import a certificate for the account. This button opens a window for specifying a certificate file (\* .cer, \* .crt). The public part of the certificate is imported to the platform. When authorizing on the server, the client's certificate is compared to the one downloaded to the database. The presence of a private key in the client's certificate is additionally checked. The import function is provided for the possibility of using custom certificate generating mechanisms.

See also: [Documentation / Platform / Platform settings / Groups / Extended authentication settings](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/docs/mt5/platform/administration/admin_groups/group_extended_authentication)

# <span id="page-203-0"></span>**Online accounts**

You can control all current connections to your trading server using the Online Users section in MetaTrader 5 Manager.

For convenience, each type of connection is indicated by a separate icon: — desktop client terminal, — Administrator terminal, — Manager terminal, — mobile terminal for Android, — signal provider (connection to the account via the signal server) etc. If the user is connected to the same account from different devices/terminals, all connections will be displayed as separate entries.

![](_page_203_Picture_4.jpeg)

In addition to trading information, the section provides technical details on account connections. The list of accounts shows the IP address from which the client is connected or the domain name if option "Enable DNS Lookup" is enabled in the context menu.

For more details, click Technical Details... in the context menu, as shown above. Next, enter the network command to execute in the Command field. You can specify your own command or choose one of the commonly used: "ping", "tracert" or "whois". After clicking Run, the result of the command execution will appear below.

To forcibly disconnect a client from the server, select Drop in the context menu. After the connection is dropped, the client terminals will automatically attempt to reconnect.

The Online Users tab can be hidden by using the "Hide online users" option in the manager terminal settings. This reduces the load on the manager terminal and the consumption of network traffic.

# <span id="page-205-0"></span>**Depositing and withdrawing funds**

The easier and faster your clients can start trading, the less likely they are to change their mind and leave. One of the key steps on this process is the ability to deposit funds. The MetaTrader 5 platform provides special tools for this. You can embed deposit and withdrawal buttons directly into trading terminals, both desktop and mobile.

Selection of these buttons will open a certain page on the broker's website. The information necessary for the deposit/withdrawal operation is passed to the website: account number and currency, name, language, client group type etc. The trader will only need to specify the amount. This allows traders to proceed instantly.

## **How it works for traders**

From the MetaTrader 5 desktop client terminal, the deposits and withdrawals requests can be made via:

- ·the context menu of the Navigator
- ·the context menu of the Trade section in the Toolbox
- ·the + icon in the account status line in in the Trade section

![](_page_205_Picture_9.jpeg)

In the MetaTrader 5 mobile terminal (both iOS and Android), the commands for fast navigation to deposit/withdrawal pages are available in the Accounts and Trading sections:

A tap on the Deposit command directly opens the broker's payment page where the trader only needs to specify the amount and select a preferred payment method. All other details, including the trader's name, trading account data and payment purpose, are automatically substituted based on the rules set by the broker. Traders will surely appreciate the efficiency, speed and automation of one-click payments.

The trading terminal does not perform any account deposit/withdrawal operations. The integrated functions redirect the user to the relevant broker's website pages.
