Links in the account allocation section apply globally to all groups. Thus, added deposit and withdrawal links will be available for all accounts within the platform (including demo accounts).

Links in groups allow overriding global settings or enabling this option only for separate accounts. If you do not want to enable deposit and withdrawal commands for all accounts, leave appropriate account allocation settings unchanged and add links only to the specific group settings.

Trader parameters separated by "&" symbol are additionally passed in the address line. Analyze the parameters to direct the traders to the required page and to automatically fill deposit/withdrawal form fields.

https://www.deposit.url/?login=1815985&email=john@smith.net&type=real&currency=USD&

acp=1252&label=MetaQuotes&server=MetaQuotes-Demo&interface=English& cid=35069eb0358e7b01023bdef53313c0cc&age=437&utm\_campaign=download& utm\_source=www.metatrader5.com

The following parameters are supported:

- ·login — account login.
- ·type — account type: demo, contest or real.
- ·currency — account deposit currency
- ·acp — the code page used by the trader.
- ·label — the name of the company which owns the terminal White Label.
- ·server — the name of the server on which the account is opened.
- ·interface — the current language of the client terminal interface.
- ·cid — the unique identifier of the trader's computer.
- ·age — the number of days since the installation of the client terminal.
- ·email — the email address specified in the trading account.
- · utm\_campaign — the name of the marketing campaign from the Lead Campaign field of the trading account. This parameter is filled automatically, in accordance with the tag specified in the terminal installation link.
- · utm\_source — the source name from the Lead Source field of the trading account This parameter is filled automatically, in accordance with the tag specified in the terminal installation link.

You can use the [lang: ... ] macro in the address line to forward a trader to the desired language page. The list of languages in ISO 639-1 format should be specified here. If one of the listed languages matches the interface language of the client terminal, it will be used for the news language. Otherwise, the first language in the list is used. Example:

https://www.deposit.url/[lang:ru|en|es]

Additional link parameters are not supported. If necessary, you can use the redirect option on your site.

For example, you use the same page for deposits and withdrawals, https://www.abcbroker/balance. If you specify it in both parameters on the MetaTrader 5 side, your site will not know which action was requested by the user. To solve this issue, set different deposit and withdrawal URLs on the platform side: https://www.abcbroker/balance/deposit and https://www.abcbroker/balance/withdraw. The user can then be redirected to the appropriate page on the platform side, while the additional parameters will be registered.

## **Performing balance operations manually**

The manager terminal allows performing balance operations: depositing and withdrawing funds, crediting, charging commissions and dividends, etc.

To conduct a balance operation, set the following parameters:

- · **Operation** — type of operation:
  - ·**Balance** — changing an account balance.
  - ·**Credit** — issuing and repaying a credit.
  - ·**Charge** — any additional charges.
  - ·**Correction** — correction of trading results.

- ·**Bonus** — bonuses. Bonuses increase the client's credit funds.
- ·**Commission** — additional commissions.
- ·**Dividend** — paying taxable dividends.
- ·**Franked dividends** — paying non-taxable dividends (tax is paid by a company).
- ·**Tax** — charging a tax.
- ·**Amount** — amount of money deposited or withdrawn from the account.
- · **Comment** — a text comment to the operation. In this field, you can choose one of the earlier created comments, or write a new one.

There are two types of directions for each operation type, depositing to an account or withdrawal. The blue button is used for depositing, the red button is used for withdrawals.

By default, the platform checks payment operations to make sure the operation will not cause the account free margin and balance to become negative. In any of the above cases, the platform will not perform the operation and will return the "No money" error. If necessary, you can disable these checks by enabling the option "Conduct balance operations without checking the free margin and the current balance on the account".

#### **Features of balance operations**

- ·All balance operations are conducted in the system as deals.
- ·It is not allowed to withdraw an amount greater than the current value of the free margin.
- · The free margin is not checked for the Correction operation. Please be careful when performing operations that reduce the balance. If the client has open positions and you write off an amount greater than the free margin, this will cause the Stop out to trigger on the account.

The "Accountant (deposit/withdraw)" permission is required for accessing the section. The permission is granted by the platform administrator.

#### **History of operations**

The history of balance operations conducted earlier is available at the bottom of the window:

- · **Time** — the time of the operation. When funds are deposited to the account, icon "+" is shown at the beginning of this field. Sign "-" is used for withdrawals.
- ·**Deal** — unique operation ID in the system.
- ·**Type** — operation type.
- ·**Amount** — operation amount.
- ·**Comment** — a comment to the operation.

To view the history of balance operations, set a period and click Request at the bottom of the window. Use the context menu to enable/disable the display of milliseconds, grid or comments in the history of operations, use the context menu.

#### **Account state**

The account status bar is displayed under the list of balance operations. It contains the following information:

- ·**Balance** — money on the account, not accounting for the results of currently open positions (deposit).
- · **Credit** — amount of credit funds provided to the client by the broker. The trading platform does not provide an ability to charge interest for credit assets.
- · **Blocked** — a client group can be set up so that a profit earned by a trader during the day cannot be used for trading (it is not included in the free margin). This blocked profit is displayed in the Blocked field. At the end of the trading day, this profit is unlocked and added to the account balance.
- ·**Equity** — equity is calculated as Balance + Credit - Commission +/- Floating profit/loss - Blocked.
- ·**Margin** — money required to cover open positions and pending orders.
- · **Free** — the following parameters are displayed here:
  - · **Free Margin** — free amount of money that can be used to maintain open positions. It is calculated as Equity - Margin. Depending on the client group settings, the equity value may or may not include floating profit, floating loss or floating profit and floating loss together.
  - ·**Margin Level** — percentage of the account equity to the margin volume (Equity / Margin \* 100).

If the clients open positions show a positive result, icon "+" is shown at the beginning of the account status line. Icon "-" is shown if the result is negative.

# <span id="page-214-0"></span>**Balance check and correction**

In the manager terminal, you can easily check the balance and the state of credit funds on the accounts based on the history of deals. Such operations can be needed after the manual history correction, for example, as a result of failures or abnormal situations.

To check balances, go to the Accounts section and enable the "Balance / Checked" and "Credit / Checked" columns via the context menu.

Two values are displayed in Balance and Credit columns: the current balance/credit funds value is displayed on the left and the value calculated based on the deals history is shown on the right.

Credit funds are checked based on Credit type deals.

The check result is also displayed in the journal. If the calculated value differs from the current one, the following message will appear in the journal:

account xxx has invalid balance: xxx.xx, valid: xxx.xx

To correct the balance/credit funds value, select the desired account and execute "Balance — Fix Balance" command in the context menu. After that the calculated balance/credit value will be inserted as the current one. The correction result will be written to the journal:

balance of account '1030390' has been fixed from 9 968.70 to 9 999.80

# <span id="page-216-0"></span>**Common errors**

- · Do not move accounts between groups with different position accounting systems (netting and hedging), especially if they have open positions. They may become incorrect.
- ·Do not forget to set passwords and to enable trading for accounts after importing.
- ·Password length requirements are configured in the account group settings.
- · Manually created accounts are not linked to the client database automatically. You should link them manually.
- · If any of the described sections of the manager terminal are not available to you, request the relevant permissions from the platform administrator.

# **Conclusion**

Congratulations! You now have the necessary knowledge to manage clients in the MetaTrader 5 platform.

You can always find additional information in the built-in documentation and articles published in the MetaQuotes Support Center.

To receive the documentary evidence of your knowledge and professional skills, pass the exams and earn your MetaTrader 5 Manager certification!

[Start certification](https://support.metaquotes.net/en/certification/courses/manager)
